+esp_err_t ads126xAdcBeginTransaction(ads126xAdcHandle_t *handle, uint32_t budgetUs)
+{

+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

@@ -1070,0 +1149,29 @@ static void ads126xAdcDecodeAdc1RegisterSnapshot(
+static uint8_t ads126xAdcSnapshotMask(uint8_t reg)
+{
+    if (reg == ADS126X_REG_POWER) {
+        return ADS126X_POWER_READ_MASK; /* RESET remains evidence. */
+    }
+    if (reg == ADS126X_REG_INTERFACE) {
+        return ADS126X_INTERFACE_CONFIG_MASK;
+    }
+    if (reg == ADS126X_REG_REFMUX) {
+        return ADS126X_REFMUX_CONFIG_MASK;
+    }
+    return 0xFFu;
+}
+
+static bool ads126xAdcSnapshotBytesEqual(const uint8_t first[16],
+                                         const uint8_t second[16],
+                                         ads126xAdcHandle_t *handle)
+{
+    for (uint8_t reg = 0u; reg < 16u; ++reg) {
+        if (((first[reg] ^ second[reg]) & ads126xAdcSnapshotMask(reg)) != 0u) {
+            handle->snapshotMismatchRegister = reg;
+            handle->snapshotExpected = first[reg];
+            handle->snapshotActual = second[reg];
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}

+esp_err_t ads126xAdcWaitReferenceReady(ads126xAdcHandle_t *handle)
+{
+    if (!handle || ads126xAdcIsQuarantined(handle)) {
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    }
+    /* C42 is 1 uF REFOUT-to-AVSS on circuit.pdf sheet 3. Datasheet 11.3
+     * specifies >=50 ms. This clock starts at the actual POWER OFF->ON write,
+     * not each MUX/PGA change. Larger/revised capacitors need board validation. */
+    while (handle->enableInternalRef) {
+        uint64_t nowUs = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time();
+        if (nowUs >= handle->referenceReadyAtUs) {
+            break;
+        }
+        if (handle->transactionDeadlineUs &&
+            handle->referenceReadyAtUs >= handle->transactionDeadlineUs) {
+            return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
+        }
+        uint64_t remaining = handle->referenceReadyAtUs - nowUs;
+        TickType_t ticks = (TickType_t)((remaining * configTICK_RATE_HZ + 999999u) / 1000000u);
+        vTaskDelay(ticks ? ticks : 1u);
+    }
+    return ESP_OK;
+}

+
+uint32_t sensorarrayScanConfigAllocateRequestId(void)
+{
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    uint32_t id = s_nextRequestId++;
+    if (s_nextRequestId == 0u) { s_nextRequestId = 1u; }
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    return id;
+}
+
+esp_err_t sensorarrayScanConfigBeginConfigurationRequest(uint32_t *outRequestId)
+{
+    if (!outRequestId) { return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG; }
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    if (s_configurationRequestId != 0u) {
+        portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    }
+    s_configurationRequestId = s_nextRequestId++;
+    if (s_nextRequestId == 0u) { s_nextRequestId = 1u; }
+    *outRequestId = s_configurationRequestId;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    return ESP_OK;
+}
+
+void sensorarrayScanConfigEndConfigurationRequest(uint32_t requestId)
+{
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    if (requestId != 0u && s_configurationRequestId == requestId) {
+        s_configurationRequestId = 0u;
+    }
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+}
+
+void sensorarrayScanConfigSetRequestCallback(sensorarrayScanConfigRequestCallback_t callback)
+{
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    s_requestCallback = callback;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+}
+
+esp_err_t sensorarrayScanConfigStageAcceptedRows(uint8_t rows, uint32_t requestId)
+{
+    if (rows < 1u || rows > 8u || requestId == 0u) { return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG; }
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    if (s_configurationRequestId != requestId || s_pendingRowsValid) {
+        portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    }
+    s_pendingRows = rows;
+    s_pendingRowsValid = true;
+    s_pendingRequestId = requestId;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    return ESP_OK;
+}
+
+void sensorarrayScanConfigRecordRejected(uint32_t requestId, uint32_t errorCode)
+{
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+    s_lastRowsRequestId = requestId;
+    s_lastRowsError = errorCode;
+    s_lastRowsTerminal = true;
+    s_lastRowsApplied = false;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_scanConfigMux);
+}

+uint8_t sensorarrayAdsRegisterCompareMask(sensorarrayAdsRegisterId_t registerId)
+{
+    switch (registerId) {
+    case SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER: return 0x03u;
+    case SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_INTERFACE: return 0x0Fu;
+    case SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_REFMUX: return 0x3Fu;
+    default: return 0xFFu;
+    }
+}

+bool sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(uint32_t frameCount, uint32_t interval,
+                                     bool mode0Valid)
+{
+    return !mode0Valid || (interval != 0u && frameCount % interval == 0u);
+}

@@ -516,0 +534,36 @@ const char *sensorarrayAdsOwnerName(sensorarrayAdsOwner_t owner)
+
+sensorarrayAdsHealthComparison_t sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthCompare(
+    const sensorarrayAdsRegisterCache_t *cache,
+    const uint8_t actual[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_COUNT],
+    uint8_t expectedPower, uint8_t expectedRefmux,
+    uint8_t expectedMode0, uint8_t expectedMode1)
+{
+    sensorarrayAdsHealthComparison_t result = {0};
+    if (!cache || !actual) {
+        result.reason = SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_CACHE;
+        return result;
+    }
+    const sensorarrayAdsRegisterId_t regs[] = {
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER, SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_REFMUX, SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_MODE0,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_MODE1};
+    const uint8_t expected[] = {0u, expectedPower, expectedRefmux, expectedMode0, expectedMode1};
+    const uint8_t masks[] = {0x10u, 0x03u, 0x3Fu, 0xFFu, 0xFFu};
+    const sensorarrayAdsHealthReason_t reasons[] = {
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_RESET, SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_POWER,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_REFMUX, SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_MODE, SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_MODE};
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < sizeof(regs) / sizeof(regs[0]); ++i) {
+        if (((actual[regs[i]] ^ expected[i]) & masks[i]) != 0u) {
+            return (sensorarrayAdsHealthComparison_t){reasons[i], regs[i], expected[i], actual[regs[i]], masks[i]};
+        }
+    }
+    for (uint8_t i = 0u; i < SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_COUNT; ++i) {
+        uint8_t mask = sensorarrayAdsRegisterCompareMask((sensorarrayAdsRegisterId_t)i);
+        if (cache->registers[i].valid &&
+            ((cache->registers[i].value ^ actual[i]) & mask) != 0u) {
+            return (sensorarrayAdsHealthComparison_t){SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_CACHE,
+                (sensorarrayAdsRegisterId_t)i, cache->registers[i].value, actual[i], mask};
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

+void sensorarrayAdsGapNoteFirstValidFrame(uint64_t frameEndUs)
+{
+    sensorarrayAdsTransactionEvidence_t *transactions[] = {
+        &s_railTransaction, &s_batteryTransaction, &s_checkTransaction};
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < 3u; ++i) {
+        sensorarrayAdsTransactionEvidence_t *t = transactions[i];
+        if (t->id && t->completedUs && !t->firstValidFrameUs &&
+            frameEndUs > t->completedUs && t->cleanupError == ESP_OK) {
+            t->firstValidFrameUs = frameEndUs;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void sensorarrayAdsTransactionBegin(sensorarrayAdsTransactionEvidence_t *t)
+{
+    uint32_t id = t->id + 1u;
+    *t = (sensorarrayAdsTransactionEvidence_t){
+        .id = id ? id : 1u, .startedUs = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time()};
+}
+
+static void sensorarrayAdsTransactionEnd(sensorarrayAdsTransactionEvidence_t *t,
+    esp_err_t originalError, esp_err_t cleanupError)
+{
+    t->originalError = originalError;
+    t->cleanupError = cleanupError;
+    t->completedUs = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time();
+    ++s_transactionGeneration;
+}

+static void sensorarrayAdsMatrixCaptureFault(sensorarrayAdsMatrixEngine_t *engine,
+    sensorarrayAdsFaultStage_t stage, esp_err_t originalError,
+    uint8_t reg, uint8_t expected, uint8_t actual, uint8_t mask)
+{
+    if (!engine || engine->faultContext.valid) {
+        return; /* Preserve the first condition before any cleanup. */
+    }
+    sensorarrayAdsMatrixFaultContext_t *fault = &engine->faultContext;
+    fault->valid = true;
+    fault->stage = stage;
+    fault->originalError = originalError;
+    fault->mode = sensorarrayMeasurementModeName(engine->mode);
+    fault->route = fault->mode;
+    fault->owner = sensorarrayAdsOwnerName(engine->registerCache.owner);
+    fault->engineFrameCount = engine->frameCount;
+    fault->modeEntryFrameCount = engine->modeEntryFrameCount;
+    fault->healthInterval = CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_HEALTH_INTERVAL;
+    fault->cacheGeneration = engine->registerCache.generation;
+    fault->physicalGeneration = engine->state->ads.configurationGeneration;
+    fault->configGeneration = sensorarrayAdsMatrixConfigurationGeneration(engine);
+    fault->registerId = reg;
+    fault->expected = expected;
+    fault->actual = actual;
+    fault->compareMask = mask;
+    sensorarrayAdsGapCopyTransactionEvidence(&fault->rail, &fault->battery, &fault->check);
+}
+
+bool sensorarrayAdsMatrixEngineCopyFaultContext(
+    const sensorarrayAdsMatrixEngine_t *engine,
+    sensorarrayAdsMatrixFaultContext_t *outContext)
+{
+    if (!engine || !outContext || !engine->faultContext.valid) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *outContext = engine->faultContext;
+    return true;
+}

@@ -226,0 +215,30 @@ uint32_t sensorarrayBatterySchedulerAgeMs(
+
+void sensorarrayBatterySchedulerRecordValidity(sensorarrayBatteryScheduler_t *scheduler,
+    bool valid, uint8_t reason, uint64_t completedUs)
+{
+    if (!scheduler) { return; }
+    if (valid) {
+        scheduler->repeatedFailureCount = 0u;
+        scheduler->lastFailureReason = 0u;
+        return;
+    }
+    if (scheduler->lastFailureReason == reason) {
+        if (scheduler->repeatedFailureCount < UINT32_MAX) {
+            ++scheduler->repeatedFailureCount;
+        }
+    } else {
+        scheduler->lastFailureReason = reason;
+        scheduler->repeatedFailureCount = 1u;
+    }
+    /* 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 periods, capped at 30 s unless the user already
+     * requested a longer period. A change of reason restarts the probe budget. */
+    if (scheduler->enabled && scheduler->repeatedFailureCount > 1u) {
+        uint32_t shift = scheduler->repeatedFailureCount - 1u;
+        if (shift > 5u) { shift = 5u; }
+        uint64_t periodUs = sensorarrayBatteryMillisecondsToMicroseconds(scheduler->periodMs);
+        uint64_t delayUs = periodUs << shift;
+        uint64_t capUs = periodUs > 30000000u ? periodUs : 30000000u;
+        if (delayUs > capUs) { delayUs = capUs; }
+        scheduler->nextDueUs = sensorarrayBatteryAddSaturating(completedUs, delayUs);
+    }
+}

@@ -1023,0 +1024,69 @@ EventBits_t sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkerDoneBit(sensorarrayFdcDeviceId_t devId)
+bool sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuiescent(void)
+{
+    return sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&s_fdcWorkers[0].lifecycle) &&
+           sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&s_fdcWorkers[1].lifecycle);
+}
+
+bool sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuarantined(void)
+{
+    return sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&s_fdcWorkers[0].lifecycle) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_QUARANTINED ||
+           sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&s_fdcWorkers[1].lifecycle) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_QUARANTINED;
+}
+
+/* A wakeup/event can be lost or coalesced. Poll the persistent phase against an
+ * absolute monotonic deadline; never extend a transaction on another wakeup. */
+static TickType_t sensorarrayFdcDeadlineTicks(uint64_t deadlineUs)
+{
+    uint64_t nowUs = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time();
+    if (nowUs >= deadlineUs) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    uint64_t ticks = ((deadlineUs - nowUs) * configTICK_RATE_HZ + 999999u) / 1000000u;
+    return ticks > (uint64_t)(portMAX_DELAY - 1u) ? portMAX_DELAY - 1u : (TickType_t)ticks;
+}
+
+static bool sensorarrayFdcWorkersWait(bool released, uint64_t deadlineUs)
+{
+    for (;;) {
+        bool complete = true;
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 2u; ++i) {
+            sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&s_fdcWorkers[i].lifecycle);
+            complete &= released ? sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&s_fdcWorkers[i].lifecycle) :
+                phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED;
+        }
+        if (complete) {
+            return true;
+        }
+        TickType_t ticks = sensorarrayFdcDeadlineTicks(deadlineUs);
+        if (ticks == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        /* Poll at one tick even if DONE notification is lost. */
+        (void)xEventGroupWaitBits(s_fdcWorkerEvents,
+            released ? SENSORARRAY_FDC_WORKER_DONE_ALL_BITS : SENSORARRAY_FDC_WORKER_ACK_ALL_BITS,
+            pdTRUE, pdFALSE, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+static bool sensorarrayFdcCancelAndReclaim(uint64_t reclaimDeadlineUs)
+{
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 2u; ++i) {
+        sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(&s_fdcWorkers[i].lifecycle);
+        (void)xTaskNotifyGive(s_fdcWorkers[i].task);
+    }
+    bool released = sensorarrayFdcWorkersWait(true, reclaimDeadlineUs);
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 2u; ++i) {
+        sensorarrayFdcWorkerContext_t *ctx = &s_fdcWorkers[i];
+        if (!sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&ctx->lifecycle)) {

diff --git a/core/measure/fdc/sensorarrayFdcWorkerLifecycle.h b/core/measure/fdc/sensorarrayFdcWorkerLifecycle.h
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e0a8817
--- /dev/null
+++ b/core/measure/fdc/sensorarrayFdcWorkerLifecycle.h
@@ -0,0 +1,95 @@
+#pragma once
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <stdint.h>
+
+/* Added in this repair: notifications are wakeups, never JOB/RUN semantics.
+ * Release/acquire publishes all persistent slot storage across the two cores. */
+typedef enum {
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_IDLE = 0,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RUNNING,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_DONE,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_REQUESTED,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_ACKED,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RECLAIMED,
+    SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_QUARANTINED,
+} sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t;
+
+typedef struct {
+    uint32_t phase;
+    uint32_t released;
+} sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t;
+
+static inline sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(
+    const sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    return (sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t)__atomic_load_n(&life->phase, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+}
+
+static inline bool sensorarrayFdcJobChange(sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life,
+    sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t from, sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t to)
+{
+    uint32_t expected = (uint32_t)from;
+    return __atomic_compare_exchange_n(&life->phase, &expected, (uint32_t)to,
+                                       false, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+}
+
+static inline bool sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(const sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    return __atomic_load_n(&life->released, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0u ||
+           sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_IDLE;
+}
+
+static inline bool sensorarrayFdcJobPost(sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life);
+    if (phase != SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_IDLE && phase != SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RECLAIMED) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    __atomic_store_n(&life->released, 0u, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
+    __atomic_store_n(&life->phase, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+    return true;
+}
+
+static inline void sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life);
+    while (phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED || phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED ||
+           phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RUNNING) {
+        if (sensorarrayFdcJobChange(life, phase, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_REQUESTED)) {
+            return;
+        }
+        phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life);
+    }
+}
+
+static inline void sensorarrayFdcJobFinish(sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life);
+    while (phase != SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_QUARANTINED) {
+        sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t terminal = phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_REQUESTED ?
+            SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_ACKED : SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_DONE;
+        if (sensorarrayFdcJobChange(life, phase, terminal)) {
+            break;
+        }
+        phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life);
+    }
+    /* Last access to job/result/trace precedes this release publication. */
+    __atomic_store_n(&life->released, 1u, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+}
+
+static inline bool sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    if (!sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(life)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    sensorarrayFdcJobPhase_t phase = sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(life);
+    return (phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_DONE || phase == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_ACKED) &&
+        sensorarrayFdcJobChange(life, phase, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RECLAIMED);
+}
+
+static inline void sensorarrayFdcJobQuarantine(sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t *life)
+{
+    __atomic_store_n(&life->phase, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_QUARANTINED, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+}

+#pragma once

+#include "sensorarrayMeasurementMode.h"
+#include "sensorarrayRowModeProfile.h"

+/* 本轮新增. Legacy gen/rid keep their original domains; this identity is
+ * captured by the acquisition owner before sampling and copied with the frame. */
+typedef struct {
+    uint64_t sessionId;
+    uint32_t bootId;
+    uint32_t revision;
+    uint32_t appliedFrameBoundary;
+    uint32_t modeGeneration;
+    uint32_t modeRequestId;
+    uint32_t rowsGeneration;
+    uint32_t rowsRequestId;
+    uint32_t profileGeneration;
+    uint32_t profileRequestId;
+    sensorarrayMeasurementState_t state;
+    sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t mode;
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfileState_t profileState;
+    uint8_t rows;

+} sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t;
+
+typedef struct {
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t identity;
+    uint32_t stateError;
+    uint32_t rowsTerminalId;
+    uint32_t rowsError;
+    sensorarrayConfigOutcome_t rowsOutcome;
+    uint32_t modeTerminalId;
+    uint32_t modeError;
+    sensorarrayConfigOutcome_t modeOutcome;
+    uint32_t profileTerminalId;
+    uint32_t profileError;
+    sensorarrayConfigOutcome_t profileOutcome;
+    bool available;
+} sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t;
+
+/* Capture does not claim the candidate was applied. Only the single Core 1
+ * owner publishes, after applying the route/profile. Readers copy one atomic
+ * authority object, never combine independent global snapshots. */
+void sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(
+    uint64_t sessionId, uint32_t bootId,
+    const sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t *mode,
+    const sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t *rows,
+    const sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t *profile,

+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *outIdentity);
+void sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(
+    const sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *identity,
+    const sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t *mode,
+    const sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t *profile,
+    const sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t *rows);
+bool sensorarrayConfigIdentityCopy(sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t *outSnapshot);
+esp_err_t sensorarrayConfigIdentityFormat(char *response, size_t responseSize);

diff --git a/core/measure/sensorarrayConfigIdentity.c b/core/measure/sensorarrayConfigIdentity.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..43a3f5a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/core/measure/sensorarrayConfigIdentity.c
@@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
+#include "sensorarrayConfigIdentity.h"
+
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
+
+static portMUX_TYPE s_authorityMux = portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED;
+static sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t s_authority;
+
+static bool sensorarrayConfigIdentitySame(const sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *a,
+                                          const sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *b)
+{
+    return a->sessionId == b->sessionId && a->bootId == b->bootId &&
+        a->modeGeneration == b->modeGeneration && a->modeRequestId == b->modeRequestId &&
+        a->rowsGeneration == b->rowsGeneration && a->rowsRequestId == b->rowsRequestId &&
+        a->profileGeneration == b->profileGeneration &&
+        a->profileRequestId == b->profileRequestId && a->state == b->state &&
+        a->mode == b->mode && a->profileState == b->profileState && a->rows == b->rows &&
+        memcmp(a->profile, b->profile, sizeof(a->profile)) == 0;
+}
+
+void sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(
+    uint64_t sessionId, uint32_t bootId,
+    const sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t *mode,
+    const sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t *rows,
+    const sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t *profile,
+    uint32_t frameBoundary,
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *outIdentity)
+{
+    if (!mode || !rows || !profile || !outIdentity) {
+        return;
+    }
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t next = {
+        .sessionId = sessionId, .bootId = bootId, .appliedFrameBoundary = frameBoundary,
+        .modeGeneration = mode->generation, .modeRequestId = mode->appliedRequestId,
+        .rowsGeneration = rows->generation, .rowsRequestId = rows->requestId,
+        .profileGeneration = profile->generation, .profileRequestId = profile->appliedRequestId,
+        .state = mode->state, .mode = mode->activeMode,
+        .profileState = profile->state, .rows = rows->rows,
+    };
+    (void)sensorarrayRowModeProfileFormat(profile->modes, next.profile);
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_authorityMux);
+    if (s_authority.available && sensorarrayConfigIdentitySame(&next, &s_authority.identity)) {
+        next.revision = s_authority.identity.revision;
+        next.appliedFrameBoundary = s_authority.identity.appliedFrameBoundary;
+    } else {
+        next.revision = s_authority.available &&
+            sessionId == s_authority.identity.sessionId ? s_authority.identity.revision + 1u : 1u;
+        if (next.revision == 0u) {
+            next.revision = 1u;
+        }
+    }
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_authorityMux);
+    *outIdentity = next;
+}
+
+void sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(
+    const sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *identity,
+    const sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t *mode,
+    const sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t *profile,
+    const sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t *rows)
+{
+    if (!identity || !mode || !profile || !rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+    sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t next = {
+        .identity = *identity,
+        .stateError = mode->lastError,
+        .rowsTerminalId = rows->lastRequestId, .rowsError = rows->lastError,
+        .rowsOutcome = !rows->lastTerminal ? SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_NONE :
+            (rows->lastApplied ? SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_APPLIED : SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_REJECTED),
+        .modeTerminalId = mode->lastRequestId, .modeError = mode->lastRequestError,
+        .modeOutcome = mode->lastOutcome,
+        .profileTerminalId = profile->lastRequestId, .profileError = profile->lastRequestError,
+        .profileOutcome = profile->lastOutcome, .available = true,
+    };
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_authorityMux);
+    s_authority = next;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_authorityMux);
+}
+
+bool sensorarrayConfigIdentityCopy(sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t *outSnapshot)
+{
+    if (!outSnapshot) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_authorityMux);
+    *outSnapshot = s_authority;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_authorityMux);
+    return outSnapshot->available;
+}
+
+static const char *sensorarrayConfigOutcomeName(sensorarrayConfigOutcome_t outcome)
+{
+    return outcome == SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_APPLIED ? "applied" :
+        (outcome == SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_REJECTED ? "rejected" : "none");
+}
+
+esp_err_t sensorarrayConfigIdentityFormat(char *response, size_t responseSize)
+{
+    if (!response || responseSize == 0u) {
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
+    }
+    sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t snapshot;
+    if (!sensorarrayConfigIdentityCopy(&snapshot)) {
+        snprintf(response, responseSize, "ERR,cmd=CONFIG,reason=not_published\n");
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    }
+    const sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *id = &snapshot.identity;
+    bool mixed = false;
+    for (size_t row = 1u; row < 8u; ++row) {
+        mixed = mixed || id->profile[row] != id->profile[0];
+    }
+    const char *modeName = mixed && sensorarrayMeasurementModeIsDataMode(id->mode) ?
+        "MIXED" : sensorarrayMeasurementModeName(id->mode);
+    int written = snprintf(response, responseSize,
+        "CONFIG,session=%016llX,bootId=%lu,rev=%lu,boundary=%lu,state=%s,mode=%s,rows=%u,profile=%s,err=0x%lX,"
+        "mgen=%lu,mrid=%lu,rgen=%lu,rrid=%lu,pgen=%lu,prid=%lu,"
+        "mtermId=%lu,mterm=%s,merr=0x%lX,ptermId=%lu,pterm=%s,perr=0x%lX,rtermId=%lu,rterm=%s,rerr=0x%lX\n",
+        (unsigned long long)id->sessionId, (unsigned long)id->bootId,
+        (unsigned long)id->revision, (unsigned long)id->appliedFrameBoundary,
+        sensorarrayMeasurementStateName(id->state), modeName,
+        (unsigned)id->rows, id->profile, (unsigned long)snapshot.stateError,
+        (unsigned long)id->modeGeneration, (unsigned long)id->modeRequestId,
+        (unsigned long)id->rowsGeneration, (unsigned long)id->rowsRequestId,
+        (unsigned long)id->profileGeneration, (unsigned long)id->profileRequestId,
+        (unsigned long)snapshot.modeTerminalId, sensorarrayConfigOutcomeName(snapshot.modeOutcome),
+        (unsigned long)snapshot.modeError, (unsigned long)snapshot.profileTerminalId,
+        sensorarrayConfigOutcomeName(snapshot.profileOutcome), (unsigned long)snapshot.profileError,
+        (unsigned long)snapshot.rowsTerminalId, sensorarrayConfigOutcomeName(snapshot.rowsOutcome),
+        (unsigned long)snapshot.rowsError);
+    if (written < 0 || (size_t)written >= responseSize) {
+        response[0] = '\0';
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;
+    }
+    return ESP_OK;
+}

diff --git a/core/transport/sensorarraySerialIo.c b/core/transport/sensorarraySerialIo.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9d48bcf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/core/transport/sensorarraySerialIo.c
@@ -0,0 +1,261 @@
+#include "sensorarraySerialIo.h"
+
+#include <errno.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include <sys/stat.h>
+#include "driver/usb_serial_jtag.h"
+#include "esp_timer.h"
+#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
+#include "esp_vfs.h"
+#include "freertos/queue.h"
+#include "freertos/task.h"
+#include "sdkconfig.h"
+#ifndef CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_OUTPUT_TASK_CORE
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_OUTPUT_TASK_CORE 0
+#endif
+#include "sensorarrayTransport.h"
+
+/* One physical TX owner. stdout/log, DATA and priority replies all enter bounded
+ * queues; no network or acquisition task calls a USB write/flush. */
+#define SERIAL_IO_QUEUE_COUNT 4u
+#define SERIAL_IO_DEADLINE_US 20000u
+#define SERIAL_IO_CHUNK 64u
+/* DATA has five slots (four queued + consumer); priority has four smaller
+ * slots. No producer allocates a full payload on its task stack. */
+static char (*s_dataBytes)[SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_TEXT_MAX];
+static uint8_t s_dataMemory; /* 0 unavailable, 1 internal, 2 PSRAM */
+static char s_priorityBytes[4][SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_CTRL_TEXT_MAX];
+static uint16_t s_lengths[9];
+static char *sensorarraySerialBytes(unsigned index)
+{
+    return index < 5u ? s_dataBytes[index] : s_priorityBytes[index - 5u];
+}
+static StaticQueue_t s_queueStorage[2];
+static uint8_t s_queueBytes[2][SERIAL_IO_QUEUE_COUNT * sizeof(uint8_t)];
+static bool s_inUse[9];
+static portMUX_TYPE s_poolMux = portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED;
+static QueueHandle_t s_queues[2];
+static TaskHandle_t s_task;
+
+static portMUX_TYPE s_statsMux = portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED;
+static sensorarraySerialIoStats_t s_stats;
+static bool s_vfsReady;
+static bool s_vfsRegistered;
+
+esp_err_t sensorarraySerialIoQueue(const char *data, size_t length, bool priority)
+{
+    if (!data || !length || length > (priority ? SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_CTRL_TEXT_MAX : SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_TEXT_MAX)) {
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;
+    }
+    if (!s_task || !s_queues[priority ? 1 : 0]) {
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    }
+    uint8_t index = UINT8_MAX;
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_poolMux);
+    for (unsigned i = priority ? 5u : 0u; i < (priority ? 9u : 5u); ++i) {
+        if (!s_inUse[i]) { s_inUse[i] = true; index = (uint8_t)i; break; }
+    }
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_poolMux);
+    bool queued = false;
+    if (index != UINT8_MAX) {
+        s_lengths[index] = (uint16_t)length;
+        memcpy(sensorarraySerialBytes(index), data, length);
+        queued = xQueueSend(s_queues[priority ? 1 : 0], &index, 0) == pdTRUE;
+        if (!queued) {
+            portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_poolMux);
+            s_inUse[index] = false;
+            portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_poolMux);
+        }
+    }
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+    if (queued) {
+        s_stats.sinkQueued++;
+        s_stats.priorityQueued += priority ? 1u : 0u;
+    } else {
+        s_stats.sinkDropped++;
+    }
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+    if (queued) {
+        (void)xTaskNotifyGive(s_task);
+    }
+    return queued ? ESP_OK : ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
+}
+
+static int sensorarraySerialOpen(const char *path, int flags, int mode)
+{
+    (void)path; (void)flags; (void)mode;
+    return 0;
+}
+static int sensorarraySerialClose(int fd) { (void)fd; return 0; }
+static int sensorarraySerialFstat(int fd, struct stat *st)
+{
+    (void)fd;
+    memset(st, 0, sizeof(*st));
+    st->st_mode = S_IFCHR;
+    return 0;
+}
+static ssize_t sensorarraySerialWrite(int fd, const void *data, size_t size)
+{
+    (void)fd;
+    if (size == 0u) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    esp_err_t err = sensorarraySerialIoQueue(data, size, false);
+    if (err != ESP_OK) {
+        errno = err == ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE ? EMSGSIZE : EAGAIN;
+        return -1;
+    }
+    return (ssize_t)size;
+}
+
+static void sensorarraySerialTxTask(void *arg)
+{
+    (void)arg;
+    bool needsDelimiter = false;
+    uint64_t lastDrainBytes = 0;
+    for (;;) {
+        (void)ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);
+        uint8_t index;
+        while (xQueueReceive(s_queues[1], &index, 0) == pdTRUE ||
+               xQueueReceive(s_queues[0], &index, 0) == pdTRUE) {
+            const char *packetData = sensorarraySerialBytes(index);
+            size_t packetLength = s_lengths[index];
+            size_t offset = 0;
+            int64_t deadline = esp_timer_get_time() + SERIAL_IO_DEADLINE_US;
+            uint64_t accepted = 0;
+            while (esp_timer_get_time() < deadline) {
+                if (needsDelimiter) {
+                    if (usb_serial_jtag_write_bytes("\n", 1u, 0) == 1) {
+                        accepted++;
+                        needsDelimiter = false;
+                    }
+                } else if (offset < packetLength) {
+                    size_t chunk = packetLength - offset;
+                    if (chunk > SERIAL_IO_CHUNK) { chunk = SERIAL_IO_CHUNK; }
+                    int written = usb_serial_jtag_write_bytes(packetData + offset, chunk, 0);
+                    if (written > 0) {
+                        offset += (size_t)written;
+                        accepted += (uint32_t)written;
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    break;
+                }
+                vTaskDelay(1);
+            }
+            int64_t nowUs = esp_timer_get_time();
+            portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+            s_stats.driverAcceptedBytes += accepted;
+            if (accepted) { s_stats.lastTxUs = nowUs; }
+            if (offset != packetLength) {
+                s_stats.timeout++;
+                s_stats.sinkDropped++;
+                s_stats.partialWrite += offset != 0u ? 1u : 0u;
+                needsDelimiter |= offset != 0u;
+            }
+            uint64_t acceptedTotal = s_stats.driverAcceptedBytes;
+            portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+            portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_poolMux);
+            s_inUse[index] = false;
+            portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_poolMux);
+            if (acceptedTotal != lastDrainBytes && usb_serial_jtag_wait_tx_done(0) == ESP_OK) {
+                lastDrainBytes = acceptedTotal;
+                portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+                s_stats.driverDrained++;
+                portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+            }
+        }
+        /* Newlib retains FILE error flags after EAGAIN; a later queue drain
+         * permits retries. These only acquire short stdio locks, never USB. */
+        clearerr(stdout);
+        clearerr(stderr);
+    }
+}
+
+esp_err_t sensorarraySerialIoInit(void)
+{
+    if (s_task && s_vfsReady) { return ESP_OK; }
+    if (!s_dataBytes) {
+        /* CPU-only payload: the driver copies each 64-byte chunk into its own
+         * internal ring buffer. Allocate once, outside every producer path. */
+        s_dataBytes = heap_caps_calloc(5u, sizeof(*s_dataBytes),
+                                      MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT);
+        if (s_dataBytes) {
+            s_dataMemory = SENSORARRAY_SERIAL_MEMORY_PSRAM;
+        } else {
+            s_dataBytes = heap_caps_calloc(5u, sizeof(*s_dataBytes),
+                                          MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT);
+            if (s_dataBytes) { s_dataMemory = SENSORARRAY_SERIAL_MEMORY_INTERNAL; }
+        }
+        if (!s_dataBytes) { return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM; }
+    }
+    if (!usb_serial_jtag_is_driver_installed()) {
+        usb_serial_jtag_driver_config_t config = {.tx_buffer_size = 1024, .rx_buffer_size = 512};
+        esp_err_t err = usb_serial_jtag_driver_install(&config);
+        if (err != ESP_OK) { return err; }
+    }
+    if (!s_task) {
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 2u; ++i) {
+            s_queues[i] = xQueueCreateStatic(SERIAL_IO_QUEUE_COUNT, sizeof(uint8_t),
+                                            s_queueBytes[i], &s_queueStorage[i]);
+            if (!s_queues[i]) { return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM; }
+        }
+        if (xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(sensorarraySerialTxTask, "serialTx", 4096, NULL, 4, &s_task,
+                                   CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_OUTPUT_TASK_CORE) != pdPASS) {
+            return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
+        }
+    }
+    if (!s_vfsReady) {
+        const esp_vfs_t vfs = {.flags = ESP_VFS_FLAG_DEFAULT, .open = sensorarraySerialOpen,
+            .write = sensorarraySerialWrite, .close = sensorarraySerialClose, .fstat = sensorarraySerialFstat};
+        if (!s_vfsRegistered) {
+            esp_err_t err = esp_vfs_register("/dev/sensorarray", &vfs, NULL);
+            if (err != ESP_OK) { return err; }
+            s_vfsRegistered = true;
+        }
+        if (!freopen("/dev/sensorarray", "w", stdout) || !freopen("/dev/sensorarray", "w", stderr)) {
+            return ESP_FAIL;
+        }
+        setvbuf(stdout, NULL, _IONBF, 0);
+        setvbuf(stderr, NULL, _IONBF, 0);
+        s_vfsReady = true;
+    }
+    return ESP_OK;
+}
+
+int sensorarraySerialIoRead(void *buffer, size_t length)
+{
+    if (!usb_serial_jtag_is_driver_installed()) { return 0; }
+    TickType_t ticks = pdMS_TO_TICKS(20u);
+    int count = usb_serial_jtag_read_bytes(buffer, length, ticks ? ticks : 1);
+    if (count > 0) {
+        int64_t nowUs = esp_timer_get_time();
+        portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+        s_stats.rxBytes += (uint32_t)count;
+        s_stats.lastRxUs = nowUs;
+        portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+    }
+    return count;
+}
+
+void sensorarraySerialIoNoteOversize(void)
+{
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+    s_stats.rxOversize++;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+}
+
+void sensorarraySerialIoGetStats(sensorarraySerialIoStats_t *outStats)
+{
+    if (!outStats) { return; }
+    portENTER_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+    *outStats = s_stats;
+    portEXIT_CRITICAL(&s_statsMux);
+    outStats->normalBufferBytes = s_dataBytes ? 5u * sizeof(*s_dataBytes) : 0u;
+    outStats->normalBufferMemory = s_dataMemory;
+    outStats->taskStackConfiguredBytes = 4096u;
+    outStats->taskStackMinBytes = s_task ? (uint32_t)uxTaskGetStackHighWaterMark(s_task) * sizeof(StackType_t) : 0u;
+    outStats->driverInstalled = usb_serial_jtag_is_driver_installed();
+    outStats->sofPresent = usb_serial_jtag_is_connected();
+}

new file mode 100644

+++ b/core/transport/sensorarrayTransportFrameMeta.c

+#include "sensorarrayTransportFrameMeta.h"
+#include <errno.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+static bool field(const char *text, const char *key, int base, uint64_t maximum, uint64_t *value)
+{
+    size_t keyLength = strlen(key);
+    const char *endLine = strpbrk(text, "\r\n");
+    if (!endLine) { endLine = text + strlen(text); }
+    for (const char *p = text; p < endLine;) {
+        const char *end = memchr(p, ',', (size_t)(endLine - p));
+        if (!end) { end = endLine; }
+        if ((size_t)(end - p) > keyLength && memcmp(p, key, keyLength) == 0) {
+            const char *start = p + keyLength;
+            if (*start == '-' || *start == '+' || *start == ' ') { return false; }
+            errno = 0;
+            char *parsedEnd;
+            unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(start, &parsedEnd, base);
+            if (errno || parsedEnd != end || parsed > maximum) { return false; }
+            *value = (uint64_t)parsed;
+            return true;
+        }
+        p = end < endLine ? end + 1 : endLine;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+static uint32_t u32(const char *text, const char *key)
+{
+    uint64_t value = 0;
+    (void)field(text, key, 10, UINT32_MAX, &value);
+    return (uint32_t)value;
+}
+
+void sensorarrayTransportParseFrameMeta(sensorarrayTransportPayloadSlot_t *slot)
+{
+    if (!slot) { return; }
+    slot->hasFrameMeta = false;
+    if (slot->length < 2 || slot->data[1] != ',' ||
+        !strchr("CVRM", slot->data[0])) { return; }
+    uint64_t rows, cells, sequence;
+    if (!field(slot->data, "seq=", 10, UINT32_MAX, &sequence) ||
+        !field(slot->data, "rows=", 10, 8, &rows) || rows < 1 ||
+        !field(slot->data, "cells=", 10, 64, &cells) || cells != rows * 8) { return; }
+    slot->frameSeq = (uint32_t)sequence;
+    slot->rows = (uint8_t)rows;
+    slot->cells = (uint8_t)cells;
+    slot->generation = u32(slot->data, slot->data[0] == 'M' ? "rgen=" : "gen=");
+    slot->requestId = u32(slot->data, slot->data[0] == 'M' ? "rrid=" : "rid=");
+    slot->sessionId = 0;
+    (void)field(slot->data, "session=", 16, UINT64_MAX, &slot->sessionId);
+    slot->bootId = u32(slot->data, "bootId=");
+    slot->configRevision = u32(slot->data, "rev=");
+    slot->modeGeneration = u32(slot->data, "mgen=");
+    slot->modeRequestId = u32(slot->data, "mrid=");
+    slot->rowsGeneration = u32(slot->data, "rgen=");
+    slot->rowsRequestId = u32(slot->data, "rrid=");
+    slot->profileGeneration = u32(slot->data, "pgen=");
+    slot->profileRequestId = u32(slot->data, "prid=");
+    slot->hasFrameMeta = true;
+}
+
+bool sensorarrayTransportBuildPreview(const sensorarrayTransportPayloadSlot_t *slot,
+                                               char *out,
+                                               size_t outSize)
+{
+    if (!slot || !slot->hasFrameMeta || !out || outSize == 0u) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    const char *data = slot->data;
+    size_t length = slot->length;
+    if (length == 0u) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    const char *firstLineEnd = memchr(data, '\n', length);
+    if (!firstLineEnd || data[0] != 'C' || data[1] != ',') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    char cLine[512];
+    size_t cLineLength = (size_t)(firstLineEnd - data);
+    if (cLineLength >= sizeof(cLine)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    memcpy(cLine, data, cLineLength);
+    cLine[cLineLength] = '\0';
+
+    uint64_t rf = 0, pf = 0, sf = 0;
+    (void)field(cLine, "rf=", 16, UINT32_MAX, &rf);
+    (void)field(cLine, "pf=", 16, UINT32_MAX, &pf);
+    (void)field(cLine, "sf=", 16, UINT32_MAX, &sf);
+
+    const char *bad = strstr(cLine, "bad=");
+    char badText[24] = "na";
+    if (bad) {
+        bad += 4u;
+        size_t index = 0u;
+        while (bad[index] != '\0' && bad[index] != ',' && index + 1u < sizeof(badText)) {
+            badText[index] = bad[index];
+            index++;
+        }
+        badText[index] = '\0';
+    }
+
+    int32_t first = 0;
+    int32_t last = 0;
+    bool firstSet = false;
+    const char *cursor = firstLineEnd + 1;
+    const char *end = data + length;
+    while (cursor < end) {
+        const char *lineEnd = memchr(cursor, '\n', (size_t)(end - cursor));
+        if (!lineEnd) {
+            break;
+        }
+        if (cursor[0] == 'D') {
+            const char *value = strchr(cursor, ',');
+            while (value && value < lineEnd) {
+                value++;
+                char *valueEnd = NULL;
+                long parsed = strtol(value, &valueEnd, 10);
+                if (valueEnd == value || valueEnd > lineEnd) {
+                    break;
+                }
+                if (!firstSet) {
+                    first = (int32_t)parsed;
+                    firstSet = true;
+                }
+                last = (int32_t)parsed;
+                value = valueEnd < lineEnd ? strchr(valueEnd, ',') : NULL;
+            }
+        }
+        cursor = lineEnd + 1;
+    }
+
+    char firstText[16] = "na", lastText[16] = "na";
+    if (firstSet) {
+        (void)snprintf(firstText, sizeof(firstText), "%ld", (long)first);
+        (void)snprintf(lastText, sizeof(lastText), "%ld", (long)last);
+    }
+    int written = snprintf(out, outSize,
+        "B20,seq=%lu,rows=%u,cfps=na,efps=na,rf=%02lX,pf=%02lX,sf=%02lX,bad=%s,d0=%s,dl=%s,previewOnly=1,gen=%lu,rid=%lu,session=%016llX,bootId=%lu,rev=%lu,mgen=%lu,mrid=%lu,rgen=%lu,rrid=%lu,pgen=%lu,prid=%lu\n",
+        (unsigned long)slot->frameSeq, (unsigned)slot->rows,
+        (unsigned long)rf, (unsigned long)pf, (unsigned long)sf, badText, firstText, lastText,
+        (unsigned long)slot->generation, (unsigned long)slot->requestId,
+        (unsigned long long)slot->sessionId, (unsigned long)slot->bootId,
+        (unsigned long)slot->configRevision, (unsigned long)slot->modeGeneration,
+        (unsigned long)slot->modeRequestId, (unsigned long)slot->rowsGeneration,
+        (unsigned long)slot->rowsRequestId, (unsigned long)slot->profileGeneration,
+        (unsigned long)slot->profileRequestId);
+    return written > 0 && (size_t)written < outSize;
+}

+        lastHealthyUs = 0u;

+        return;

+        healthyFrames++;
+    }

+        healthyFrames < SENSORARRAY_BOOT_STABLE_PROGRESS_MIN_FRAMES) {
+        return;
+    }

+/* 本轮新增: only the acquisition owner publishes this atomic authority. */
+static sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t sensorarrayPublishConfigAuthority(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx, uint32_t frameBoundary)
+{
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t identity = {.sessionId = s_configSessionId};
+    sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t mode = {0};
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t profile = {0};
+    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumb_t boot = {0};
+    bool bootKnown = sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbCopy(&boot);
+    if (bootKnown) { identity.bootId = boot.bootId; }
+    if (!ctx || !sensorarrayMeasurementModeCopySnapshot(&ctx->measurementMode, &mode) ||
+        !sensorarrayRowModeProfileCopy(&ctx->rowModeProfile, &profile) ||
+        !bootKnown) {
+        return identity;
+    }
+    sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t rows = sensorarrayScanConfigGetFrameSnapshot();
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(s_configSessionId, boot.bootId, &mode, &rows,
+                                     &profile, frameBoundary, &identity);
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(&identity, &mode, &profile, &rows);
+    return identity;
+}
+
+static void sensorarrayCaptureFrameIdentity(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx,
+    const sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t *mode,
+    const sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t *profile,
+    uint32_t frameBoundary, sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *outIdentity)
+{
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t candidate = *profile;
+    if (candidate.pending) {
+        memcpy(candidate.modes, candidate.pendingModes, sizeof(candidate.modes));
+        candidate.generation++;
+        if (candidate.generation == 0u) { candidate.generation = 1u; }
+        candidate.appliedRequestId = candidate.pendingRequestId;
+        candidate.state = SENSORARRAY_ROW_PROFILE_STATE_APPLIED;
+    }
+    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumb_t boot = {0};
+    if (sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbCopy(&boot)) {
+        sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(s_configSessionId, boot.bootId, mode,
+            &ctx->scanPlan.configSnapshot, &candidate, frameBoundary, outIdentity);
+    }
+}

diff --git a/main/output/sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage.h b/main/output/sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage.h
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f97708c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/main/output/sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage.h
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <stddef.h>
+#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
+
+/* 本轮新增: startup-owned, permanent CPU-only formatting/frame storage. No DMA,
+ * queue control, or critical-section object may be placed in this allocation. */
+typedef struct {
+    void *memory;
+    size_t bytes;
+    bool usesPsram;
+} sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t;
+
+/* Called only by the single startup owner, before the log task is created.
+ * A failed allocation leaves a retryable empty state. A successful allocation
+ * is never replaced (including after a subsequent task-create failure). */
+static inline bool sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(
+    sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t *storage, size_t bytes, bool preferPsram)
+{
+    if (!storage || bytes == 0u) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (storage->memory) {
+        return storage->bytes == bytes;
+    }
+    void *memory = NULL;
+    bool usesPsram = false;
+    if (preferPsram) {
+        memory = heap_caps_calloc(1u, bytes, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT);
+        usesPsram = memory != NULL;
+    }
+    if (!memory) {
+        memory = heap_caps_calloc(1u, bytes, MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT);
+    }
+    if (!memory) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    storage->memory = memory;
+    storage->bytes = bytes;
+    storage->usesPsram = usesPsram;
+    return true;
+}
diff --git a/main/output/sensorarrayTextProtocol.c b/main/output/sensorarrayTextProtocol.c
index d6171c9..90e504f 100644
--- a/main/output/sensorarrayTextProtocol.c
+++ b/main/output/sensorarrayTextProtocol.c
@@ -32,0 +33,32 @@ static size_t sensorarrayTextAppend(char *buffer,
+/* 本轮新增: extend only from the immutable frame, never from live state.
+ * K repeats the session/revision; its CRC covers the complete extended header. */
+static size_t sensorarrayTextAppendIdentity(char *buffer, size_t size, size_t position,
+                                            const sensorarrayFrame_t *frame,
+                                            bool trailer)
+{
+    if (frame->identity.revision == 0u || position == 0u || position >= size) {
+        return position;
+    }
+    if (buffer[position - 1u] != '\n') {
+        return size;
+    }
+    position--;
+    const sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t *id = &frame->identity;
+    position = sensorarrayTextAppend(buffer, size, position,
+        ",session=%016llX,bootId=%lu,rev=%lu",
+        (unsigned long long)id->sessionId, (unsigned long)id->bootId,
+        (unsigned long)id->revision);
+    if (!trailer) {
+        position = sensorarrayTextAppend(buffer, size, position,
+            ",mgen=%lu,mrid=%lu", (unsigned long)id->modeGeneration,
+            (unsigned long)id->modeRequestId);
+        if (!frame->mixedProfile) {
+            position = sensorarrayTextAppend(buffer, size, position,
+                ",rgen=%lu,rrid=%lu,pgen=%lu,prid=%lu",
+                (unsigned long)id->rowsGeneration, (unsigned long)id->rowsRequestId,
+                (unsigned long)id->profileGeneration, (unsigned long)id->profileRequestId);
+        }
+    }
+    return sensorarrayTextAppend(buffer, size, position, "\n");
+}
+

diff --git a/tests/fixtures/ads_fault/README.md b/tests/fixtures/ads_fault/README.md
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b4d0f0b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/fixtures/ads_fault/README.md
@@ -0,0 +1,9 @@
+# Synthetic ADS fault replays
+
+These are synthetic contexts emitted by the **production** `sensorarrayAdsFaultFormat`, not E1–E7 field logs. Their timestamp, boot, sequence and register values are explicit fixture inputs. No truncated original line was repaired or replaced.
+
+Regenerate with `python3 tools/test_ads_fault_fixtures.py --update`, then verify with `python3 tools/test_ads_fault_fixtures.py`. Both were actually executed successfully. The script compiles the C formatter and emitter with the host compiler, checks first-error/pre-cleanup mode preservation, separate cleanup error, per-record 384-byte bounds, attempted/acquired flags and exact fixture equality.
+
+These diagnostic text records have no ASCII measurement CRC. Measurement golden frames and actual encoder CRCs are separate under `tests/fixtures/config_identity`.
+
+`reg` is the register-cache index for stable health comparisons (POWER=0, INTERFACE=1, MODE0=2, MODE1=3, MODE2=4, INPMUX=5, REFMUX=6, OFCAL0..2=7..9, FSCAL0..2=10..12). For REGISTER_READ_UNSTABLE it is the physical register address of the first disagreeing byte. ROW_GPIO_COMPARE uses the selected row. OWNER_ACQUIRE expected/actual are owner enum values. This is diagnosis metadata and must be interpreted with `stage`.

+# 合成协议回放夹具
+
+本目录不是现场实录，不能当作 E1–E7 的硬件复现证据。
+
+`*.frame` 是 `tests/host/test_config_identity.c` 使用真实固件 `sensorarrayTextProtocolBuildFrame()` 输出；`*.config` 使用真实 `sensorarrayConfigIdentityFormat()` 输出。运行 `python3 tools/test_config_identity.py` 会重新生成并独立验证 CRC。原始现场日志没有被改写或补全。
+
+- `cap_before → volt_transition → cap_after → cap_reapplied`：CAP→VOLT→CAP、同一 CAP 新请求的连续配置；C legacy gen 始终来自 ROWS，explicit mgen 递增。
+- 丢失 MAPP：只投递 cap_after.frame，然后查询 cap_after.config。禁止将 cap_before.config 的身份盖到新帧。
+- 数据早于 MAPP：按上一顺序投递，查询匹配后解除 pending；Applied 不证明 PC 已收到过有效值。
+- 旧终态/旧 epoch 迟到：已经确认 cap_reapplied.config 后，再投递 cap_before 或 cap_after 帧/关联请求终态；不能倒退模式状态。
+- `health_before_conversion`：合成全帧 acquired=0 的健康前置失败。它并不是“64 次 ADC 转换失败”的实录。
+- `*_1`、`*_8`：CAP/VOLT/RES/mixed 的 1/8 行 encoder 黄金样本。
+- `res_extreme`、`mixed_extreme`：合法 12 位负 fixed value 和最大整数 metadata，分别 1844、2085 bytes，实际 CRC 正常。不是实际可施加的电压/电阻测试条件。
+
+脚本执行五类状态恢复对照断言，但不替代真实 Host UI 的集成/HIL 测试。

@@ -0,0 +1,67 @@
+/* Synthetic contexts serialized by the production fault formatter. */
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "sensorarrayAdsFault.h"
+
+int main(void)
+{
+    const sensorarrayAdsFaultStage_t stages[] = {
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_REGISTER_HEALTH_READ,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_REGISTER_READ_UNSTABLE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_POWER_COMPARE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_REFMUX_COMPARE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_MODE_COMPARE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_RESET_OBSERVED,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_CACHE_COMPARE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_ROW_GPIO_COMPARE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_OWNER_ACQUIRE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_RESTORE_VERIFY,
+    };
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < sizeof(stages) / sizeof(stages[0]); ++i) {
+        sensorarrayAdsMatrixFaultContext_t context = {
+            .valid = true, .stage = stages[i],
+            .originalError = i == 0u ? ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT : ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE,
+            .cleanupError = i == 2u || i == 9u ? ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE : ESP_OK,
+            .mode = "VOLT", .route = "VOLT", .owner = "MATRIX",
+            .engineFrameCount = 128u, .modeEntryFrameCount = 65u,
+            .healthInterval = 64u, .healthReadbackDue = true,
+            .precisionFrame = true, .registerId = 0u,
+            .expected = 2u, .actual = i == 5u ? 0x12u : 3u,
+            .compareMask = i == 5u ? 0x10u : 0x03u,
+            .cacheGeneration = 14u, .physicalGeneration = 29u, .configGeneration = 42u,
+            .rail = {.id = 4u, .startedUs = 1000000u, .completedUs = 1004900u,
+                     .firstValidFrameUs = 1018200u},
+            .battery = {.id = 19u, .startedUs = 2000000u, .completedUs = 2005600u,
+                        .originalError = ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE},
+            .check = {.id = 2u, .startedUs = 3000000u, .completedUs = 3001000u},
+        };
+        if (i == 0u) { context.compareMask = 0u; context.expected = context.actual = 0u; }
+        if (i == 1u) { context.registerId = 3u; context.expected = 0u; context.actual = 1u; context.compareMask = 0xFFu; }
+        if (i == 3u || i == 9u) { context.registerId = 6u; context.expected = 0x24u; context.actual = 0u; context.compareMask = 0x3Fu; }
+        if (i == 4u) { context.registerId = 2u; context.expected = 0u; context.actual = 1u; context.compareMask = 0xFFu; }
+        if (i == 5u) { context.expected = 0u; }
+        if (i == 6u) { context.registerId = 5u; context.expected = 0x08u; context.actual = 0x18u; context.compareMask = 0xFFu; }
+        if (i == 7u) { context.registerId = 1u; context.expected = 0u; context.actual = 1u; context.compareMask = 0x07u; }
+        if (i == 8u) { context.expected = 0u; context.actual = 3u; context.compareMask = 0xFFu; }
+        sensorarrayAdsFaultEvent_t event = {
+            /* Deliberately post-cleanup values: captured context must win. */
+            .stage = SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_MATRIX_ROUTE,
+            .err = ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE,
+            .bootId = 123456u, .bootCount = 7u, .seq = 512u,
+            .modeGeneration = 4u, .profileGeneration = 3u,
+            .rowGeneration = 2u, .rowRequestId = 1u,
+            .mode = "NONE", .route = "SAFE", .owner = "NONE",
+            .profile = "VVVVVVVV", .drdyGeneration = 100u, .configGeneration = 42u,
+            .railSpanUv = 5200000, .avddUv = 3400000, .avssUv = -1800000,
+            .railSource = "external_calibration", .railValid = true,
+            .reference = "avdd-avss", .context = &context,
+        };
+        char text[SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_LINE_MAX + 1u];
+        size_t length = sensorarrayAdsFaultFormat(&event, text, sizeof(text));
+        if (!length || length != strlen(text)) { return 1; }
+        printf("# fixture=%s; synthetic=1; producer=sensorarrayAdsFaultFormat; not_field_capture=1\n",
+            sensorarrayAdsFaultStageName(stages[i]));
+        fwrite(text, 1u, length, stdout);
+    }
+    return 0;
+}

+#undef printf
+static void *threadMain(void *arg)
+{
+    sensorarrayFdcWorkerContext_t *ctx = arg;
+    currentTask = ctx->task;
+    sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkerTask(ctx);
+    return NULL;
+}
+int main(void)
+{
+    for (scenario = 0; scenario <= 6; ++scenario) {
+        memset(s_fdcWorkers, 0, sizeof(s_fdcWorkers));
+        memset(runCount, 0, sizeof(runCount)); memset(consumed, 0, sizeof(consumed));
+        eventBits = SENSORARRAY_FDC_WORKER_DONE_ALL_BITS; /* stale wakeup */
+        TestTask tasks[2] = {0};
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
+            tasks[i].id = i;
+            pthread_mutex_init(&tasks[i].mutex, NULL); pthread_cond_init(&tasks[i].cond, NULL);
+            s_fdcWorkers[i].task = &tasks[i]; s_fdcWorkers[i].devId = i; s_fdcWorkers[i].initialized = true;
+            appState.devices[i].ready = true; appState.devices[i].handle = &appState;
+            pthread_create(&tasks[i].thread, NULL, threadMain, &s_fdcWorkers[i]);
+        }
+        unsigned repeats = scenario == 0 ? 50u : 1u;
+        for (unsigned iteration = 1; iteration <= repeats; ++iteration) {
+            sensorarrayFdcAutoscanSamples_t outputs[2] = {{0xdead}, {0xbeef}};
+            sensorarrayFdcRuntimeChannelConfig_t configs[2][4] = {0};
+            sensorarrayFdcDeviceTiming_t timing[2] = {0};
+            sensorarrayFdcRowTiming_t row = {0};
+            sensorarrayFdcFrameReadTracker_t tracker = {.frameSeq = iteration};
+            bool timeout = false;
+            int64_t start = esp_timer_get_time();
+            esp_err_t err = sensorarrayMeasureReadFdcMatrixRowParallelEpoch(&appState, 1, iteration, iteration,
+                &outputs[0], &outputs[1], configs, &timing[0], &timing[1], &row, &tracker, &timeout);
+            assert(esp_timer_get_time() - start < 85000); /* fixed 40ms+20ms reclaim, scheduler margin */
+            if (scenario == 0 || scenario == 4) {
+                assert(err == ESP_OK && outputs[0].value == iteration && outputs[1].value == iteration);
+                assert(sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuiescent());
+                assert(!sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuarantined());
+            } else if (scenario == 5) {
+                assert(err != ESP_OK && runCount[0] == 0 && runCount[1] == 0);
+                assert(sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuiescent());
+            } else {
+                assert(err != ESP_OK && sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuarantined());
+                assert(!sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuiescent()); /* cannot perform mode transition */
+                assert(outputs[0].value == 0xdead && outputs[1].value == 0xbeef);
+                /* Returning does not return any worker buffer to a stack. */
+                outputs[0].value = 0x1234; outputs[1].value = 0x5678;
+                delayUs(100000);
+                assert(sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuiescent());
+                assert(sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuarantined()); /* late release cannot re-use slot */
+                assert(outputs[0].value == 0x1234 && outputs[1].value == 0x5678);
+                assert(sensorarrayMeasureReadFdcMatrixRowParallelEpoch(&appState, 1, 999, 999,
+                    &outputs[0], &outputs[1], configs, &timing[0], &timing[1], &row, &tracker, &timeout) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+            }
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
+            pthread_mutex_lock(&tasks[i].mutex); tasks[i].stop = true; pthread_cond_signal(&tasks[i].cond); pthread_mutex_unlock(&tasks[i].mutex);
+            pthread_join(tasks[i].thread, NULL); pthread_mutex_destroy(&tasks[i].mutex); pthread_cond_destroy(&tasks[i].cond);
+        }
+    }
+    puts("FDC_WORKER_RUNTIME_PASS,actualFunctions=worker+coordinator,iterations=56,asan=1,ubsan=1,scenarios=normal50;job_unconsumed;ack_delay;run_delay;done_lost;cancel_before_run;blocked_bus;mode_guard;stale_done");
+    return 0;
+}

+static uint32_t ulTaskNotifyTake(int clear, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    TestTask *task = currentTask;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&task->mutex);
+    while (!task->notifications && !task->stop) {
+        if (ticks == portMAX_DELAY) { pthread_cond_wait(&task->cond, &task->mutex); }
+        else {
+            struct timespec until; clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &until);
+            until.tv_nsec += (long)ticks * 1000000;
+            until.tv_sec += until.tv_nsec / 1000000000; until.tv_nsec %= 1000000000;
+            if (pthread_cond_timedwait(&task->cond, &task->mutex, &until) != 0) { break; }
+        }
+    }
+    if (task->stop) { pthread_mutex_unlock(&task->mutex); pthread_exit(NULL); }
+    unsigned result = task->notifications;
+    if (clear) { task->notifications = 0; } else if (result) { task->notifications--; }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&task->mutex);
+    if (result && task->id == 1 && scenario == 1 && consumed[1]++ == 0) { delayUs(90000); }
+    if (result && task->id == 1 && scenario == 3 && sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&s_fdcWorkers[1].lifecycle) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RUNNING) { delayUs(90000); }
+    return result;
+}
+static EventBits_t xEventGroupSetBits(int events, EventBits_t bits)
+{
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&eventMutex);
+    if (scenario != 4 || !(bits & SENSORARRAY_FDC_WORKER_DONE_ALL_BITS)) { eventBits |= bits; }
+    EventBits_t value = eventBits; pthread_mutex_unlock(&eventMutex); return value;
+}
+static EventBits_t xEventGroupClearBits(int events, EventBits_t bits)
+{
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&eventMutex); EventBits_t value = eventBits; eventBits &= ~bits; pthread_mutex_unlock(&eventMutex); return value;
+}
+static EventBits_t xEventGroupWaitBits(int events, EventBits_t bits, int clear, int all, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    delayUs(ticks * 1000);
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&eventMutex); EventBits_t result = eventBits;
+    if (clear) { eventBits &= ~bits; }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&eventMutex); return result;
+}

+/* Synthetic fault injection against the actual production driver. No HIL claims. */
+#include <assert.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "../../components/ads126xAdc/ads126xAdc.c"
+
+static int64_t nowUs;
+static uint32_t lockDelayUs, queueDelayUs, completionDelayUs;
+static bool mutexAvailable = true, completionBlocked, wrongDescriptor;
+static unsigned queueCalls, freeCalls, snapshotReads;
+static uint8_t registers[32], snapshots[3][32];
+static bool useSnapshots;
+static esp_err_t queueError;
+static spi_transaction_t *pending;
+static int64_t completeAtUs;
+static ads126xAdcHandle_t *notifyHandle;
+static bool notifyEdge, notifyConfigChange;
+
+int64_t esp_timer_get_time(void) { return nowUs; }
+void *heap_caps_malloc(size_t size, int flags) { (void)flags; return malloc(size); }
+void heap_caps_free(void *ptr) { ++freeCalls; free(ptr); }
+bool esp_ptr_dma_capable(const void *ptr) { return ptr != NULL; }
+SemaphoreHandle_t xSemaphoreCreateMutex(void) { return (void *)1; }
+void vSemaphoreDelete(SemaphoreHandle_t handle) { (void)handle; }
+BaseType_t xSemaphoreTake(SemaphoreHandle_t handle, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    (void)handle;
+    uint64_t budget = (uint64_t)ticks * 1000u;
+    if (!mutexAvailable || lockDelayUs > budget) { nowUs += budget; return pdFALSE; }
+    nowUs += lockDelayUs; return pdTRUE;
+}
+BaseType_t xSemaphoreGive(SemaphoreHandle_t handle) { (void)handle; return pdTRUE; }
+TaskHandle_t xTaskGetCurrentTaskHandle(void) { return (void *)1; }
+BaseType_t xTaskNotifyStateClear(TaskHandle_t task) { (void)task; return pdTRUE; }
+uint32_t ulTaskNotifyTake(BaseType_t clear, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    (void)clear;
+    if (!ticks) { return 0u; }
+    if (notifyEdge && notifyHandle) {
+        nowUs += 100u;
+        ++notifyHandle->drdyGeneration;
+        if (notifyConfigChange) { ++notifyHandle->configurationGeneration; }
+        notifyEdge = false;
+        return 1u;
+    }
+    nowUs += (int64_t)ticks * 1000; return 0u;
+}
+void vTaskDelay(TickType_t ticks) { nowUs += (int64_t)ticks * 1000; }
+TickType_t xTaskGetTickCount(void) { return (TickType_t)(nowUs / 1000); }
+void vTaskNotifyGiveFromISR(TaskHandle_t task, BaseType_t *woken) { (void)task; *woken = pdFALSE; }
+esp_err_t gpio_set_direction(gpio_num_t pin, int mode) { (void)pin; (void)mode; return ESP_OK; }
+esp_err_t gpio_set_level(gpio_num_t pin, int level) { (void)pin; (void)level; return ESP_OK; }

+esp_err_t spi_device_queue_trans(spi_device_handle_t device, spi_transaction_t *trans, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    (void)device; ++queueCalls;
+    assert(ticks != portMAX_DELAY);
+    if (queueError != ESP_OK) { return queueError; }
+    uint64_t budget = (uint64_t)ticks * 1000u;
+    if (queueDelayUs > budget) { nowUs += budget; return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT; }
+    nowUs += queueDelayUs;
+    assert(pending == NULL);
+    pending = trans;
+    completeAtUs = nowUs + completionDelayUs;
+    return ESP_OK;
+}
+
+esp_err_t spi_device_get_trans_result(spi_device_handle_t device, spi_transaction_t **out, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    (void)device;
+    assert(ticks != portMAX_DELAY);
+    uint64_t budget = (uint64_t)ticks * 1000u;
+    if (!pending || completionBlocked || completeAtUs - nowUs > (int64_t)budget) {
+        nowUs += budget; return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
+    }
+    if (completeAtUs > nowUs) { nowUs = completeAtUs; }
+    if (wrongDescriptor) {
+        static spi_transaction_t wrong;
+        *out = &wrong; return ESP_OK;
+    }
+    const uint8_t *tx = pending->tx_buffer;
+    uint8_t *rx = pending->rx_buffer;
+    size_t bytes = pending->length / 8u;
+    memset(rx, 0, bytes);
+    if ((tx[0] & 0xE0u) == 0x20u) {
+        uint8_t reg = tx[0] & 0x1Fu;
+        size_t len = tx[1] + 1u;
+        const uint8_t *values = registers;
+        if (useSnapshots && reg == 0u && len == 16u) {
+            values = snapshots[snapshotReads < 3u ? snapshotReads : 2u];
+            ++snapshotReads;
+        }
+        memcpy(rx + 2, values + reg, len);
+    } else if ((tx[0] & 0xE0u) == 0x40u) {
+        uint8_t reg = tx[0] & 0x1Fu;
+        memcpy(registers + reg, tx + 2, tx[1] + 1u);
+    }
+    *out = pending; pending = NULL;
+    return ESP_OK;
+}
+
+static ads126xAdcHandle_t handle(void)
+{
+    nowUs = 1000000;
+    lockDelayUs = queueDelayUs = completionDelayUs = 0u;
+    mutexAvailable = true; completionBlocked = wrongDescriptor = useSnapshots = false;
+    queueError = ESP_OK; pending = NULL; snapshotReads = 0u;
+    notifyHandle = NULL; notifyEdge = notifyConfigChange = false;
+    memset(registers, 0, sizeof(registers));
+    registers[1] = 2u; registers[2] = 6u; registers[5] = 0x8Fu; registers[15] = 0x24u;
+    ads126xAdcHandle_t result = {0};
+    result.spiDevice = (void *)1; result.mutex = (void *)1;
+    result.spiTxBuf = malloc(64u); result.spiRxBuf = malloc(64u);
+    result.spiBufSize = 64u; result.spiDmaCapable = true;
+    result.drdyGpio = 1; result.resetGpio = 2; result.drdyNotificationReady = true;
+    result.enableStatusByte = true; result.crcMode = ADS126X_CRC_CRC8;
+    result.pgaGain = 1u; result.vrefMicrovolts = 5200000u;
+    return result;
+}
+
+static void testSpiDeadlineAndLifetime(void)
+{
+    ads126xAdcHandle_t ads = handle();
+    uint8_t value = 0u;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadPowerRegister(&ads, &value) == ESP_OK && value == 2u);
+    assert(!ads.spiInFlight);
+    lockDelayUs = 90000u; queueDelayUs = 8000u; completionDelayUs = 3000u;
+    int64_t start = nowUs;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadPowerRegister(&ads, &value) == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT);
+    assert(nowUs - start == 100000u);
+    assert(pending == &ads.dmaTransaction && ads.spiInFlight && ads.spiQuarantined);
+    uint8_t prior[64]; memcpy(prior, ads.spiTxBuf, sizeof(prior));
+    unsigned count = queueCalls;
+    assert(ads126xAdcWritePowerRegister(&ads, 1u) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    assert(queueCalls == count && memcmp(prior, ads.spiTxBuf, sizeof(prior)) == 0);
+    completionBlocked = true;
+    unsigned freed = freeCalls;
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE && freeCalls == freed);
+    assert(ads126xAdcHardwareReset(&ads) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    ads126xAdcConfig_t cfg = {.spiDevice = (void *)1};
+    assert(ads126xAdcInit(&ads, &cfg) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    completionBlocked = false; nowUs += 1000000;
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_OK && freeCalls == freed + 2u);
+
+    ads = handle(); queueDelayUs = 200000u;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadPowerRegister(&ads, &value) == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT);
+    assert(!ads.spiInFlight && !ads.spiQuarantined && pending == NULL);
+    queueDelayUs = 0u;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadPowerRegister(&ads, &value) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_OK);
+
+    ads = handle();
+    assert(ads126xAdcBeginTransaction(&ads, 5000u) == ESP_OK);
+    queueDelayUs = 2000u; completionDelayUs = 2000u;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadPowerRegister(&ads, &value) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadPowerRegister(&ads, &value) == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT);
+    assert(nowUs == 1005000); /* second call did not restart owner budget */
+    ads126xAdcEndTransaction(&ads);
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_OK);
+}
+
+static uint8_t crcPolynomialDivision(uint32_t value)
+{
+    uint64_t dividend = (uint64_t)value << 8u;
+    for (int bit = 39; bit >= 8; --bit) {
+        if (dividend & (UINT64_C(1) << bit)) {
+            dividend ^= UINT64_C(0x107) << (bit - 8);
+        }
+    }
+    return (uint8_t)dividend;
+}
+
+static void testCrcAndLength(void)
+{
+    ads126xAdcHandle_t ads = handle();
+    const uint32_t words[] = {0u, 1u, UINT32_MAX, 0x12345678u, 0x80000000u};
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < sizeof(words) / sizeof(words[0]); ++i) {
+        uint32_t word = words[i];
+        uint8_t frame[] = {0x40u, word >> 24u, word >> 16u, word >> 8u, word, crcPolynomialDivision(word)};
+        int32_t raw = 42; uint8_t status = 0u;
+        assert(ads126xAdcParseAdc1Frame(&ads, frame, 6u, &raw, &status) == ESP_OK);
+        assert((uint32_t)raw == word && status == 0x40u);
+        for (unsigned bit = 0u; bit < 32u; ++bit) {
+            frame[1u + bit / 8u] ^= 1u << (bit % 8u);
+            raw = 42;
+            assert(ads126xAdcParseAdc1Frame(&ads, frame, 6u, &raw, &status) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_CRC);
+            assert(raw == 42); /* bad data never escapes into range/cache */
+            frame[1u + bit / 8u] ^= 1u << (bit % 8u);
+        }
+        assert(ads126xAdcParseAdc1Frame(&ads, frame, 5u, &raw, &status) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE);
+        frame[0] ^= ADS126X_STATUS_RESET_OCCURRED;
+        assert(ads126xAdcParseAdc1Frame(&ads, frame, 6u, &raw, &status) == ESP_OK);
+        assert((status & ADS126X_STATUS_RESET_OCCURRED) != 0u); /* STATUS isn't in CRC */
+    }
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_OK);
+}
+
+static void testReferenceAndFreshness(void)
+{
+    ads126xAdcHandle_t ads = handle();
+    assert(ads126xAdcSetInternalReference(&ads, true) == ESP_OK);
+    uint64_t readyAt = ads.referenceReadyAtUs;
+    assert(readyAt == (uint64_t)nowUs + 50000u);
+    nowUs += 1000;
+    assert(ads126xAdcSetInternalReference(&ads, true) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(ads.referenceReadyAtUs == readyAt);
+    assert(ads126xAdcStartAdc1(&ads) == ESP_OK && (uint64_t)nowUs >= readyAt);
+    int64_t stable = nowUs;
+    assert(ads126xAdcStartAdc1(&ads) == ESP_OK && nowUs == stable);
+    assert(ads126xAdcSetInternalReference(&ads, false) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(ads126xAdcSetInternalReference(&ads, true) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(ads126xAdcBeginTransaction(&ads, 1000u) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(ads126xAdcStartAdc1(&ads) == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT);
+    ads126xAdcEndTransaction(&ads);
+
+    uint32_t generation = ads.drdyGeneration;
+    /* GPIO held low is insufficient, no new generation. */
+    assert(ads126xAdcWaitDrdyGenerationUs(&ads, generation, 600u, NULL) == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT);
+    notifyHandle = &ads; notifyEdge = true;
+    assert(ads126xAdcWaitDrdyGenerationUs(&ads, generation, 600u, NULL) == ESP_OK);
+    generation = ads.drdyGeneration;
+    notifyEdge = true; notifyConfigChange = true;
+    assert(ads126xAdcWaitDrdyGenerationUs(&ads, generation, 600u, NULL) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_OK);
+}
+
+static void testSnapshots(void)
+{
+    ads126xAdcHandle_t ads = handle();
+    for (unsigned i = 0u; i < 3u; ++i) { memcpy(snapshots[i], registers, sizeof(registers)); }
+    snapshots[0][1] |= 0xE0u; snapshots[0][2] |= 0xF0u; snapshots[0][15] |= 0xC0u;
+    useSnapshots = true;
+    ads126xAdc1RegisterSnapshot_t snapshot = {0};
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadAdc1RegisterSnapshot(&ads, &snapshot) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(snapshot.power == 2u && snapshot.interface == 6u && snapshot.refmux == 0x24u);
+    snapshotReads = 0u;
+    snapshots[0][3] = 1u; snapshots[1][3] = 2u; snapshots[2][3] = 3u;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadAdc1RegisterSnapshot(&ads, &snapshot) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE);
+    assert(ads.snapshotReadFailure == 2u && ads.snapshotMismatchRegister == 3u);
+    queueError = ESP_FAIL;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadAdc1RegisterSnapshot(&ads, &snapshot) == ESP_FAIL);
+    assert(ads.snapshotReadFailure == 1u);
+    queueError = ESP_OK; useSnapshots = false;
+    registers[1] |= ADS126X_POWER_RESET;
+    assert(ads126xAdcReadAdc1RegisterSnapshot(&ads, &snapshot) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(snapshot.power & ADS126X_POWER_RESET);
+    assert(ads126xAdcRestoreAdc1RegisterSnapshot(&ads, &snapshot, 5200000u, false) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    assert(ads126xAdcDeinit(&ads) == ESP_OK);
+}
+
+int main(void)
+{
+    testSpiDeadlineAndLifetime();
+    testCrcAndLength();
+    testReferenceAndFreshness();
+    testSnapshots();
+    puts("ADS_DRIVER_RELIABILITY_TESTS,passed=1,source=production_driver,fixture=synthetic");
+    return 0;
+}
diff --git a/tests/host/test_async_log_storage.c b/tests/host/test_async_log_storage.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7e22827
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/host/test_async_log_storage.c
@@ -0,0 +1,102 @@
+#include <assert.h>
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <stdint.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage.h"
+
+static bool psramAvailable, internalAvailable;
+static unsigned allocations;
+static uint32_t capabilities[8];
+
+void *heap_caps_calloc(size_t count, size_t size, uint32_t caps)
+{
+    assert(count == 1u && size != 0u && (caps & MALLOC_CAP_8BIT));
+    assert(allocations < 8u);
+    capabilities[allocations++] = caps;
+    if (((caps & MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM) && psramAvailable) ||
+        ((caps & MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL) && internalAvailable)) {
+        return calloc(count, size);
+    }
+    return NULL;
+}
+
+static void reset(bool psram, bool internal)
+{
+    psramAvailable = psram;
+    internalAvailable = internal;
+    allocations = 0u;
+    memset(capabilities, 0, sizeof(capabilities));
+}
+
+int main(void)
+{
+    const size_t bytes = 13536u;
+    sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t storage = {0};
+    reset(true, true);
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, true));
+    assert(storage.memory && storage.usesPsram && storage.bytes == bytes);
+    assert(allocations == 1u && capabilities[0] == (MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT));
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < bytes; ++i) { assert(((unsigned char *)storage.memory)[i] == 0u); }
+    void *original = storage.memory;
+    memset(original, 0xA5, bytes);
+    /* Retry after task-create failure / repeated calls never allocate or clear. */
+    for (unsigned i = 0u; i < 10000u; ++i) {
+        assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, false));
+    }
+    assert(allocations == 1u && storage.memory == original && storage.usesPsram);
+    assert(((unsigned char *)storage.memory)[bytes - 1u] == 0xA5);
+    assert(!sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes + 1u, true));
+    assert(allocations == 1u && storage.memory == original);
+    free(storage.memory);
+
+    storage = (sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t){0}; reset(false, true);
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, true));
+    assert(!storage.usesPsram && allocations == 2u);
+    assert(capabilities[1] == (MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT));
+    free(storage.memory);
+
+    storage = (sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t){0}; reset(false, false);
+    assert(!sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, true));
+    assert(!storage.memory && storage.bytes == 0u && !storage.usesPsram && allocations == 2u);
+    psramAvailable = true; /* memory later available: bounded retry succeeds */
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, true));
+    assert(storage.memory && storage.usesPsram && allocations == 3u);
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, true) && allocations == 3u);
+    free(storage.memory);
+
+    storage = (sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t){0}; reset(true, true);
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, bytes, false));
+    assert(!storage.usesPsram && allocations == 1u);
+    assert(capabilities[0] == (MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT));
+    free(storage.memory);
+    /* First pool succeeds, second fails: retry must only allocate the missing
+     * pool, preserving the first pool and any data already stored there. */
+    sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t workspace = {0}, framePool = {0};
+    reset(true, false);
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&workspace, bytes, true));
+    original = workspace.memory;
+    memset(original, 0x5A, bytes);
+    psramAvailable = false;
+    assert(!sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&framePool, 28608u, true));
+    assert(allocations == 3u && !framePool.memory);
+    internalAvailable = true;
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&workspace, bytes, true));
+    assert(allocations == 3u && workspace.memory == original);
+    assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&framePool, 28608u, true));
+    assert(allocations == 5u && framePool.memory && !framePool.usesPsram);
+    assert(((unsigned char *)workspace.memory)[bytes - 1u] == 0x5A);
+    assert(((unsigned char *)framePool.memory)[28607u] == 0u);
+    for (unsigned i = 0u; i < 10000u; ++i) {
+        assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&workspace, bytes, true));
+        assert(sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&framePool, 28608u, true));
+    }
+    assert(allocations == 5u);
+    free(workspace.memory); free(framePool.memory);
+    assert(!sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(NULL, bytes, true));
+    storage = (sensorarrayAsyncLogStorage_t){0};
+    assert(!sensorarrayAsyncLogStorageEnsure(&storage, 0u, true));
+    puts("ASYNC_LOG_STORAGE_TESTS,passed=1,psram=1,fallback=1,allfail=1,retry=1,noHotAlloc=1,secondPoolRetry=1");
+    return 0;
+}
diff --git a/tests/host/test_config_identity.c b/tests/host/test_config_identity.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6f8a1fc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/host/test_config_identity.c
