+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "sensorarrayConfigIdentity.h"
+#include "sensorarrayFrameBuilder.h"
+#include "sensorarrayTypes.h"
+#include "sensorarrayTextProtocol.h"
+
+#define CHECK(x) do { if (!(x)) { fprintf(stderr, "FAIL,line=%d,%s\n", __LINE__, #x); return 1; } } while (0)
+
+static sensorarrayFrame_t frame;
+static sensorarrayTextPacket_t packet;
+
+static int testIdentity(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayMeasurementModeContext_t mode;
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t profile;
+    sensorarrayMeasurementModeInit(&mode);
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfileInit(&profile, SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE);
+    sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t rows = {.rows=1, .cells=8, .rowMask=1, .generation=2, .requestId=1};
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t captured;
+    for (uint32_t i=0; i<50; ++i) {
+        sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t target = (i%3 == 1) ? SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_VOLTAGE : SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE;
+        CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeAccept(&mode, target, i+10));
+        CHECK(!sensorarrayMeasurementModeAccept(&mode, target, 999));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeBeginTransition(&mode));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeCompleteTransition(&mode, 8953+i, 10));
+        sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t uniform[8];
+        for (unsigned row=0;row<8;++row) { uniform[row]=target; }
+        CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept(&profile,uniform,i+10));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileCompleteTransition(&profile,8953+i,10));
+        sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(UINT64_C(0xAABBCCDDEE112233), 7, &mode.snapshot, &rows, &profile, 8953+i, &captured);
+        CHECK(captured.modeRequestId == i+10 && captured.rowsGeneration == 2);
+        sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(&captured, &mode.snapshot, &profile, &rows);
+        sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t authority;
+        CHECK(sensorarrayConfigIdentityCopy(&authority));
+        CHECK(authority.identity.revision == i+1);
+        CHECK(authority.identity.modeGeneration == mode.snapshot.generation);
+        CHECK(authority.modeTerminalId == i+10);
+        char text[512];
+        CHECK(sensorarrayConfigIdentityFormat(text, sizeof(text)) == ESP_OK);
+        CHECK(strstr(text, "rgen=2,rrid=1") && strstr(text, "mterm=applied"));
+    }
+    /* A lost/delayed MAPP can be recovered by the atomic query. The C rows
+     * generation is deliberately lower than the MODE generation. */
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t queued = captured;
+    rows.generation++; rows.requestId=99; rows.rows=8; rows.cells=64; rows.rowMask=255;
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(UINT64_C(0xAABBCCDDEE112233), 7, &mode.snapshot, &rows, &profile, 9003, &captured);
+    CHECK(captured.modeGeneration == queued.modeGeneration && captured.revision == queued.revision+1);
+    CHECK(queued.rowsGeneration == 2); /* Already queued frame is immutable. */
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(&captured, &mode.snapshot, &profile, &rows);
+    uint32_t appliedId = mode.snapshot.appliedRequestId;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeAccept(&mode, SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_RESISTANCE, 900));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeBeginTransition(&mode));
+    sensorarrayMeasurementModeFailTransition(&mode, 0x108, 11);
+    CHECK(mode.snapshot.appliedRequestId == appliedId && mode.snapshot.lastRequestId == 900);
+    CHECK(mode.snapshot.lastOutcome == SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_REJECTED);
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(UINT64_C(0xAABBCCDDEE112233), 7, &mode.snapshot, &rows, &profile, 9004, &captured);
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(&captured, &mode.snapshot, &profile, &rows);
+    char text[512];
+    CHECK(sensorarrayConfigIdentityFormat(text, sizeof(text)) == ESP_OK);
+    CHECK(strstr(text,"mtermId=900,mterm=rejected,merr=0x108"));
+    /* Profile failure also preserves the successful applied ID. */
+    sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t modes[8];
+    CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileParse("CVRCVRCV",8,modes));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept(&profile,modes,901));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileCompleteTransition(&profile,9004,10));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept(&profile,modes,902));
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfileFailTransition(&profile,0x108,10);
+    CHECK(profile.appliedRequestId == 901 && profile.lastRequestId == 902);
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfileRejectRequest(&profile,904,0x103);
+    CHECK(profile.appliedRequestId==901 && profile.lastRequestId==904 && profile.lastRequestError==0x103);
+    /* Recovery re-applies CAP with a new generation; wrap skips unknown zero. */
+    mode.snapshot.generation=UINT32_MAX;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeAccept(&mode,SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE,903));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeBeginTransition(&mode));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayMeasurementModeCompleteTransition(&mode,9005,10));
+    CHECK(mode.snapshot.generation == 1);
+    /* A cold boot can repeat legacy bootId; the new session resets revision. */
+    sensorarrayMeasurementModeInit(&mode);
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityCapture(UINT64_C(0xAABBCCDDEE112234), 7, &mode.snapshot, &rows, &profile, 0, &captured);
+    CHECK(captured.revision == 1 && captured.sessionId != queued.sessionId);
+    return 0;
+}
+
+static void seedFrame(unsigned rows, sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t mode)
+{
+    sensorarrayFrameBuilderInitInvalid(&frame);
+    frame.sequence=9198; frame.timestampUs=123456789; frame.activeRows=rows;
+    frame.configSnapshot=(sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t){.rows=rows,.cells=rows*8,.rowMask=rows==8?255:1,.generation=2,.requestId=1};
+    frame.measurement.mode=mode; frame.measurement.modeGeneration=4; frame.measurement.modeRequestId=3;
+    frame.measurement.unit=mode==SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE?SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_UNIT_PF:mode==SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_VOLTAGE?SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_UNIT_VOLT:SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_UNIT_OHM;
+    frame.measurement.decimalScale=mode==SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_RESISTANCE?-3:-6;
+    frame.expectedMask=rows==8?UINT64_MAX:255;
+    frame.acquiredMask=frame.expectedMask; frame.capValidMask=frame.expectedMask;
+    frame.measurement.validMask=frame.expectedMask; frame.measurement.freshMask=frame.expectedMask;
+    frame.measurement.errorMask=0; frame.errorMask=0; frame.freshMask=frame.expectedMask;
+    frame.validCount=rows*8; frame.freshFrame=true;
+    frame.rowProfileGeneration=5; frame.rowProfileRequestId=3;
+    frame.identity=(sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t){.sessionId=UINT64_C(0xAABBCCDDEE112233),.bootId=7,.revision=8,
+      .modeGeneration=4,.modeRequestId=3,.rowsGeneration=2,.rowsRequestId=1,.profileGeneration=5,.profileRequestId=3};
+    for(unsigned i=0;i<64;++i) { frame.capTotalPf[i]=12.25+i; frame.measurement.valuesFixed[i]=50000+i; frame.measurement.pgaGain[i]=1; }
+    for(unsigned i=0;i<8;++i) {frame.rowMode[i]=mode;frame.rowScale[i]=frame.measurement.decimalScale;}
+}
+
+static int saveFrame(const char *directory,const char *name)
+{
+    CHECK(sensorarrayTextProtocolBuildFrame(&frame,&packet)==ESP_OK);
+    char path[1024]; snprintf(path,sizeof(path),"%s/%s.frame",directory,name);
+    FILE *file=fopen(path,"wb"); CHECK(file); CHECK(fwrite(packet.data,1,packet.length,file)==packet.length); CHECK(fclose(file)==0);
+    printf("GOLDEN,name=%s,bytes=%u\n",name,(unsigned)packet.length);
+    sensorarrayMeasurementModeSnapshot_t mode={.state=sensorarrayMeasurementStateForMode(frame.measurement.mode),
+        .activeMode=frame.measurement.mode,.generation=frame.identity.modeGeneration,.appliedRequestId=frame.identity.modeRequestId,
+        .lastRequestId=frame.identity.modeRequestId,.lastOutcome=SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_APPLIED};
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t profile;
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfileInit(&profile,frame.measurement.mode);
+    memcpy(profile.modes,frame.rowMode,sizeof(profile.modes));
+    profile.generation=frame.identity.profileGeneration;profile.appliedRequestId=frame.identity.profileRequestId;
+    profile.lastRequestId=profile.appliedRequestId;profile.lastOutcome=SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_APPLIED;
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t id=frame.identity;
+    id.mode=mode.activeMode;id.state=mode.state;id.rows=frame.activeRows;id.appliedFrameBoundary=frame.sequence;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileFormat(profile.modes,id.profile));
+    frame.configSnapshot.lastTerminal=true;frame.configSnapshot.lastApplied=true;
+    frame.configSnapshot.lastRequestId=frame.configSnapshot.requestId;
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentityPublish(&id,&mode,&profile,&frame.configSnapshot);
+    char query[512];CHECK(sensorarrayConfigIdentityFormat(query,sizeof(query))==ESP_OK);
+    snprintf(path,sizeof(path),"%s/%s.config",directory,name);
+    file=fopen(path,"wb");CHECK(file);CHECK(fwrite(query,1,strlen(query),file)==strlen(query));CHECK(fclose(file)==0);
+    return 0;
+}
+
+int main(int argc,char **argv)
+{
+    CHECK(argc==2); CHECK(testIdentity()==0);
+    printf("IDENTITY_MEMORY,frame=%zu,identity=%zu,authority=%zu,packet=%zu\n",sizeof(frame),sizeof(frame.identity),sizeof(sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t),sizeof(packet));
+    for(unsigned rows=1;rows<=8;rows+=7) {
+        const char *names[]={"cap","volt","res"};
+        for(unsigned mode=0;mode<3;++mode) {seedFrame(rows,(sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t)mode);char name[32];snprintf(name,sizeof(name),"%s_%u",names[mode],rows); CHECK(saveFrame(argv[1],name)==0);}
+        seedFrame(rows,SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE);frame.mixedProfile=true;
+        for(unsigned r=0;r<8;++r){frame.rowMode[r]=(sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t)(r%3);frame.rowScale[r]=r%3==2?-3:-6;}
+        char name[32];snprintf(name,sizeof(name),"mixed_%u",rows);CHECK(saveFrame(argv[1],name)==0);
+    }
+    for (unsigned stage=0;stage<4;++stage) {
+        sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t mode=stage==1?SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_VOLTAGE:SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE;
+        seedFrame(1,mode);
+        frame.sequence=9197+stage;
+        frame.identity.revision=6+stage;
+        frame.identity.modeGeneration=frame.measurement.modeGeneration=2+stage;
+        frame.identity.modeRequestId=frame.measurement.modeRequestId=1+stage;
+        frame.identity.profileGeneration=frame.rowProfileGeneration=3+stage;

diff --git a/tests/host/test_fdc_worker_lifecycle.c b/tests/host/test_fdc_worker_lifecycle.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..eef04c0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/host/test_fdc_worker_lifecycle.c
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+#include <assert.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include "sensorarrayFdcWorkerLifecycle.h"
+
+int main(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayFdcJobLifecycle_t life = {0};
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&life));
+    /* JOB not consumed: cancellation is state, unaffected by merged wakeups. */
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(&life);
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobFinish(&life);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&life) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_CANCEL_ACKED);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    /* ACK delayed; cancellation wins without entering RUN. */
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(&life);
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RUNNING));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobFinish(&life);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    /* RUN delayed, and a stale DONE wakeup cannot release or reuse storage. */
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED));
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(&life);
+    sensorarrayFdcJobFinish(&life);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    /* DONE event lost: persistent release is sufficient evidence. */
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED));
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RUNNING));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobFinish(&life);
+    sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(&life);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&life) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_DONE);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    /* Blocked bus: quarantine preserves storage even after a late release. */
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_POSTED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED));
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobChange(&life, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_ACKED, SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_RUNNING));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobCancel(&life);
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&life));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobQuarantine(&life);
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    sensorarrayFdcJobFinish(&life);
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobReleased(&life));
+    assert(sensorarrayFdcJobPhase(&life) == SENSORARRAY_FDC_JOB_QUARANTINED);
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobPost(&life));
+    assert(!sensorarrayFdcJobReclaim(&life));
+    puts("FDC_WORKER_LIFECYCLE_PASS,scenarios=job_unconsumed;ack_delay;run_delay;done_lost;cancel_race;blocked_bus;late_done");
+    return 0;
+}

+{ boot->bootId=17;return true; }

+{ (void)route; snapshot->fdcSdHigh=false;return s_routeKnown; }

+{ (void)route;(void)reason;s_safeCalls++;return ESP_OK; }

+{
+    (void)id;
+    if (length>=sizeof(s_terminal))return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;
+    memcpy(s_terminal,text,length);s_terminal[length]='\0';s_terminalCalls++;
+    (void)sensorarrayConfigIdentityCopy(&s_terminalAuthority);
+    return ESP_OK;
+}
+static esp_err_t sensorarrayApplyMeasurementMode(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx,
+    sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t mode,uint32_t id,bool emitEvent)
+{
+    (void)emitEvent;s_applyCalls++;
+    if (!sensorarrayMeasurementModeAccept(&ctx->measurementMode,mode,id) ||
+        !sensorarrayMeasurementModeBeginTransition(&ctx->measurementMode))return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+    if (s_applyFail) {
+        sensorarrayMeasurementModeFailTransition(&ctx->measurementMode,ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE,2);
+        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE;
+    }
+    return sensorarrayMeasurementModeCompleteTransition(&ctx->measurementMode,ctx->hostFrameSequence+1,2)?ESP_OK:ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
+}
+static bool injectedAccept(sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t *profile,const sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t modes[8],uint32_t id)
+{ return !s_acceptFail && sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept(profile,modes,id); }

+/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_PUBLISH */
+/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_TERMINAL */

+#define sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept injectedAccept
+static void runMode(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx,sensorarrayCommand_t command)
+{
+    for(unsigned once=0;once<1;++once) {
+        switch(command.type) {
+/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_MODE_CASE */
+        default: break;
+        }
+    }
+}
+#undef sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept

+static void runMixedFailure(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx,
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t rowProfile,
+    const sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t effectiveModes[8],
+    uint32_t frameSequence,uint32_t mixedFailureErr)
+/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_MIXED_FAILURE */
+
+static void reset(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx)
+{
+    memset(ctx,0,sizeof(*ctx));sensorarrayMeasurementModeInit(&ctx->measurementMode);
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfileInit(&ctx->rowModeProfile,SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE);
+    (void)sensorarrayMeasurementModeAccept(&ctx->measurementMode,SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE,7);
+    (void)sensorarrayMeasurementModeBeginTransition(&ctx->measurementMode);
+    (void)sensorarrayMeasurementModeCompleteTransition(&ctx->measurementMode,100,2);
+    ctx->hostFrameSequence=100;
+    s_routeKnown=true;s_acceptFail=false;s_applyFail=false;s_applyCalls=0;s_safeCalls=0;s_terminalCalls=0;s_terminal[0]='\0';
+    s_configSessionId++;
+    (void)sensorarrayPublishConfigAuthority(ctx,101);
+}

+int main(void)
+{
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigInit()==ESP_OK);
+    sensorarrayAppContext_t ctx;
+    sensorarrayCommand_t command={.type=SENSORARRAY_COMMAND_MEASUREMENT_MODE,.value=SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_VOLTAGE,.requestId=8};
+    reset(&ctx);s_routeKnown=false;runMode(&ctx,command);
+    CHECK(s_applyCalls==0 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"MERR,",5)==0);
+    CHECK(ctx.measurementMode.snapshot.appliedRequestId==7 && ctx.rowModeProfile.appliedRequestId==0);
+    reset(&ctx);s_acceptFail=true;runMode(&ctx,command);
+    CHECK(s_applyCalls==0 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"MERR,",5)==0);
+    CHECK(ctx.measurementMode.snapshot.appliedRequestId==7);
+    reset(&ctx);ctx.measurementMode.snapshotVersion=1;runMode(&ctx,command);
+    CHECK(s_applyCalls==0 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"MERR,",5)==0);
+    CHECK(strstr(s_terminal, "identityKnown=0")!=NULL && strstr(s_terminal, "session=0000000000000000")==NULL);
+    reset(&ctx);ctx.rowModeProfile.snapshotVersion=1;runMode(&ctx,command);
+    CHECK(s_applyCalls==0 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"MERR,",5)==0);
+    reset(&ctx);s_applyFail=true;runMode(&ctx,command);
+    CHECK(s_applyCalls==1 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"MERR,",5)==0);
+    CHECK(ctx.measurementMode.snapshot.appliedRequestId==7 && ctx.rowModeProfile.appliedRequestId==0);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.modeTerminalId==8 && s_terminalAuthority.profileTerminalId==8);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.identity.state==SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_STATE_SAFE);
+    reset(&ctx);runMode(&ctx,command);
+    CHECK(s_applyCalls==1 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"MAPP,",5)==0);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.identity.modeRequestId==8 && s_terminalAuthority.identity.profileRequestId==8);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.identity.revision==2 && s_terminalAuthority.identity.appliedFrameBoundary==101);
+    CHECK(strstr(s_terminal,",rev=2,boundary=101,identityKnown=1\n")!=NULL);
+    CHECK(strcmp(s_terminalAuthority.identity.profile,"VVVVVVVV")==0);
+    reset(&ctx);
+    sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t mixed[8];CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileParse("CVRCVRCV",8,mixed));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileAccept(&ctx.rowModeProfile,mixed,9));
+    sensorarrayRowModeProfile_t pending;CHECK(sensorarrayRowModeProfileCopy(&ctx.rowModeProfile,&pending));
+    runMixedFailure(&ctx,pending,mixed,101,ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE);
+    CHECK(s_safeCalls==1 && s_terminalCalls==1 && strncmp(s_terminal,"RMERR,",6)==0);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.identity.state==SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_STATE_DEGRADED);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.identity.mode==SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_NONE);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.profileTerminalId==9 && s_terminalAuthority.profileOutcome==SENSORARRAY_CONFIG_OUTCOME_REJECTED);
+    CHECK(s_terminalAuthority.identity.profileRequestId==0);
+    CHECK(strstr(s_terminal,",rev=2,boundary=101,identityKnown=1\n")!=NULL);
+    puts("MAIN_CONFIG_COMMIT,passed=7,source=production_branches_with_hardware_stubs");
+    return 0;
+}

+static int testRegisterHealthBoundariesAndFaults(void)
+{
+    const uint32_t intervals[] = {64u, 128u};
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < 2u; ++i) {
+        for (uint32_t frame = 1u; frame <= 512u; ++frame) {
+            CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(frame, intervals[i], true) ==
+                  (frame % intervals[i] == 0u));
+        }
+        /* Mode entered at cumulative count=65: boundaries remain absolute. */
+        CHECK(!sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(65u, intervals[i], true));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(128u, intervals[i], true));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(256u, intervals[i], true));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(384u, intervals[i], true));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(512u, intervals[i], true));
+        CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(65u, intervals[i], false));
+    }
+    CHECK(!sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(UINT32_MAX, 64u, true));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(0u, 64u, true));
+    CHECK(!sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthDue(5u, 0u, true));
+    sensorarrayAdsRegisterCache_t cache;
+    sensorarrayAdsRegisterCacheInit(&cache);
+    uint8_t values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_COUNT] = {0};
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER] = 2u;
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_REFMUX] = 0x24u;
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_COUNT; ++i) {
+        sensorarrayAdsRegisterCacheNoteWrite(&cache, (sensorarrayAdsRegisterId_t)i, values[i], true);
+    }
+    CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthCompare(&cache, values, 2u, 0x24u, 0u, 0u).reason == SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_OK);
+    const sensorarrayAdsRegisterId_t regs[] = {
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER, SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_REFMUX, SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_MODE0,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_MODE1, SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_MODE2,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_FSCAL2};
+    const uint8_t changes[] = {0x10u, 1u, 1u, 1u, 1u, 1u, 1u};
+    const sensorarrayAdsHealthReason_t reasons[] = {
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_RESET, SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_POWER,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_REFMUX, SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_MODE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_MODE, SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_CACHE,
+        SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_CACHE};
+    for (size_t i = 0u; i < sizeof(regs) / sizeof(regs[0]); ++i) {
+        values[regs[i]] ^= changes[i];
+        sensorarrayAdsHealthComparison_t result = sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthCompare(
+            &cache, values, 2u, 0x24u, 0u, 0u);
+        CHECK(result.reason == reasons[i] && result.reg == regs[i]);
+        CHECK(((result.actual ^ result.expected) & result.mask) != 0u);
+        values[regs[i]] ^= changes[i];
+    }
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER] |= 0xE0u;
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_REFMUX] |= 0xC0u;
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_INTERFACE] |= 0xF0u;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthCompare(&cache, values, 2u, 0x24u, 0u, 0u).reason == SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_OK);
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_POWER] |= 0x10u;
+    values[SENSORARRAY_ADS_REGISTER_REFMUX] ^= 1u;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayAdsRegisterHealthCompare(&cache, values, 2u, 0x24u, 0u, 0u).reason == SENSORARRAY_ADS_HEALTH_RESET);
+    return 0;
+}

+static int testBatteryBoundedBackoff(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayBatteryScheduler_t scheduler;
+    sensorarrayBatterySchedulerInit(&scheduler, true, 1000u, 3000u, 0u);
+    for (uint32_t i = 0u; i < 40u; ++i) {
+        uint64_t now = scheduler.nextDueUs;
+        sensorarrayBatterySchedulerRecordRun(&scheduler, now, 5000u, true, true);
+        sensorarrayBatterySchedulerRecordValidity(&scheduler, false, 7u, now);
+        CHECK(scheduler.nextDueUs > now);
+        CHECK(scheduler.nextDueUs - now <= 30000000u);
+    }
+    sensorarrayBatterySchedulerRequestNow(&scheduler, scheduler.lastRunUs + 1u);
+    CHECK(sensorarrayBatterySchedulerIsDue(&scheduler, scheduler.lastRunUs + 1u));
+    sensorarrayBatterySchedulerRecordValidity(&scheduler, true, 0u, scheduler.lastRunUs);
+    CHECK(scheduler.repeatedFailureCount == 0u);
+    return 0;
+}

+static int testAdsFaultPreCleanupAndBounds(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayAdsMatrixFaultContext_t context = {
+        .valid = true, .stage = SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_REGISTER_HEALTH_READ,
+        .originalError = ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT, .cleanupError = ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE,
+        .mode = "VOLT", .route = "VOLT", .owner = "MATRIX",
+        .engineFrameCount = UINT32_MAX, .modeEntryFrameCount = UINT32_MAX,
+        .healthInterval = UINT32_MAX, .cacheGeneration = UINT32_MAX,
+        .physicalGeneration = UINT32_MAX, .configGeneration = UINT32_MAX,
+        .registerId = 255u, .expected = 255u, .actual = 255u, .compareMask = 255u,
+        .healthReadbackDue = true, .precisionFrame = true,
+    };
+    sensorarrayAdsTransactionEvidence_t txn = {
+        .id = UINT32_MAX, .startedUs = UINT64_MAX, .completedUs = UINT64_MAX,
+        .firstValidFrameUs = UINT64_MAX, .originalError = ESP_FAIL, .cleanupError = ESP_FAIL};
+    context.rail = txn; context.battery = txn; context.check = txn;
+    sensorarrayAdsFaultEvent_t event = {
+        .stage = SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_MATRIX_ROUTE, .err = ESP_ERR_INVALID_RESPONSE,
+        .bootId = UINT32_MAX, .bootCount = UINT32_MAX, .seq = UINT32_MAX,
+        .modeGeneration = UINT32_MAX, .profileGeneration = UINT32_MAX,
+        .rowGeneration = UINT32_MAX, .rowRequestId = UINT32_MAX, .attempt = UINT32_MAX,
+        .mode = "NONE", .route = "SAFE", .owner = "NONE", .context = &context,
+    };
+    char line[SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_LINE_MAX + 1u];
+    size_t len = sensorarrayAdsFaultFormat(&event, line, sizeof(line));
+    CHECK(len > 0u);
+    CHECK(strstr(line, "stage=REGISTER_HEALTH_READ,err=0x107") != NULL);
+    CHECK(strstr(line, "mode=VOLT") != NULL && strstr(line, "route=SAFE") == NULL);
+    CHECK(strstr(line, "originalError=0x107,cleanupError=0x108") != NULL);
+    CHECK(strstr(line, "attempted=0,acquired=0") != NULL);
+    const char *start = line;
+    while (*start) {
+        const char *end = strchr(start, '\n');
+        CHECK(end && (size_t)(end - start) + 1u < 384u);
+        start = end + 1u;
+    }

+    CHECK(sink.calls == 7u && sink.length == len);
+    context.stage = SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_POWER_COMPARE;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayAdsFaultEmit(&event, 9000001u, sensorarrayAdsFaultTestSink, &sink) > 0u);
+    context.stage = SENSORARRAY_ADS_FAULT_STAGE_REGISTER_READ_UNSTABLE;
+    context.mode = "1234567890123456"; context.route = context.mode; context.owner = context.mode;

+    event.configGeneration = UINT32_MAX; event.reference = context.mode;
+    event.railSource = "123456789012345678901234";

+
+static void setExhaustedBudget(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbMarkStableProgress(false);
+    s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount = SENSORARRAY_BOOT_AUTO_RESTART_MAX;
+    s_bootBreadcrumb.guardState = SENSORARRAY_BOOT_GUARD_RECOVERY_SAFE;
+    s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartWindowStartUs = mockRtcUs;
+}
+
+static void healthyAt(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx, uint64_t second)
+{
+    mockMonoUs = second * 1000000;
+    ctx->frame.freshFrame = true;
+    actualFrameProgress(ctx, ESP_OK, 1);
+}
+
+static void testRecoveryIdentity(void)
+{
+    mockAuthorityKnown = true;
+    mockAuthority.identity = (sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t){
+        .sessionId = UINT64_C(0x1234567812345678), .bootId = 7, .revision = 10,
+        .modeGeneration = 4, .modeRequestId = 3, .rowsGeneration = 2,
+        .rowsRequestId = 1, .profileGeneration = 6, .profileRequestId = 5,
+    };
+    sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t initial = mockAuthority.identity;
+    uint32_t id = 0, level = SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_NONE, actualId = 0;
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_FULL, &id));
+    assert(id == 1);
+    uint32_t rejectedId = UINT32_MAX;
+    assert(!sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_SOFT, &rejectedId));
+    assert(rejectedId == UINT32_MAX && s_recoveryRequestId == id);
+    mockAuthority.identity.revision++;
+    mockAuthority.identity.state++; /* fault state alone may change revision */
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId));
+    assert(level == SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_FULL && actualId == id && staleErrors == 0);
+    assert(!sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId));
+
+    for (unsigned domain = 0; domain < 7; ++domain) {
+        mockAuthority.identity = initial;
+        assert(sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_FULL, &id));
+        switch (domain) {
+        case 0: mockAuthority.identity.sessionId++; mockAuthority.identity.bootId++; break;
+        case 1: mockAuthority.identity.modeGeneration++; break;
+        case 2: mockAuthority.identity.modeRequestId++; break;
+        case 3: mockAuthority.identity.rowsGeneration++; break;
+        case 4: mockAuthority.identity.rowsRequestId++; break;
+        case 5: mockAuthority.identity.profileGeneration++; break;
+        case 6: mockAuthority.identity.profileRequestId++; break;
+        }
+        unsigned before = staleErrors;
+        assert(!sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId));
+        assert(level == SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_NONE && actualId == id);
+        assert(staleErrors == before + 1 && strstr(lastLog, "reason=stale_config"));
+    }
+    mockAuthorityKnown = false;
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_FULL, &id));
+    mockAuthorityKnown = true;
+    assert(!sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId)); /* unknown -> known */
+    assert(staleErrors == 8);
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_FULL, &id));
+    mockAuthorityKnown = false;
+    assert(!sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId)); /* known -> unknown */
+    assert(staleErrors == 9);
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_RESTART, &id));
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId)); /* unknown -> unknown boot recovery */
+    assert(level == SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_RESTART && actualId == id);
+    s_recoveryRequestSeq = UINT32_MAX;
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryPost(SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_FULL, &id));
+    assert(id == 1); /* reserved zero is skipped at finite counter wrap */
+    assert(sensorarrayRecoveryTake(&level, &actualId));
+    assert(lockDepth == 0);
+    puts("RECOVERY_IDENTITY_PASS,productionFunctions=post+take,singlePending=1,copyOutsideMux=1,revisionOnlyAllowed=1,staleSessionAndDomains=7,knownTransitions=1,unknownBootRecovery=1,idWrap=1");
+}
+
+int main(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayAppContext_t ctx = {0};
+    mockResetReason = ESP_RST_POWERON;
+    mockRtcUs = 1000000;
+    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbStart();
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+
+    /* Real RTC breadcrumb and boot-start logic preserve budget across slow
+     * software restarts. Each startup exceeds the old 30-second window. */
+    for (unsigned i = 1; i <= SENSORARRAY_BOOT_AUTO_RESTART_MAX; ++i) {
+        assert(sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart(SENSORARRAY_BOOT_RESTART_KIND_AUTO));
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == i);
+        uint64_t firstAttemptUs = s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartWindowStartUs;
+        uint32_t previousBootId = s_bootBreadcrumb.bootId;
+        mockRtcUs += (30ULL * 1000000ULL) * 2;
+        mockMonoUs = (30ULL * 1000000ULL) * 2;
+        mockResetReason = ESP_RST_SW;
+        sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbStart();
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.bootId == previousBootId + 1);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == i);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartWindowStartUs == firstAttemptUs);
+        actualReadyBlock(&ctx, ESP_OK, ESP_OK);
+        assert(ctx.systemReady && s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == i);
+    }
+    mockRtcUs += 3600ULL * 1000000;
+    assert(!sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart(SENSORARRAY_BOOT_RESTART_KIND_AUTO));
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.guardState == SENSORARRAY_BOOT_GUARD_RECOVERY_SAFE);
+    actualReadyBlock(&ctx, ESP_OK, ESP_OK);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.guardState == SENSORARRAY_BOOT_GUARD_RECOVERY_SAFE);
+    assert(readyEvents == 4);
+
+    /* Idle time and READY cannot grant a fourth auto restart. */
+    mockMonoUs += 3600ULL * 1000000;
+    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbMarkStableProgress(false);
+    assert(!sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart(SENSORARRAY_BOOT_RESTART_KIND_AUTO));
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    assert(sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart(SENSORARRAY_BOOT_RESTART_KIND_MANUAL));
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3); /* explicit action, not fabricated health */
+
+    /* Exercise the exact production call site with plenty of healthy frames:
+     * 119 seconds is insufficient even after exceeding 128 true frames. */
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 1);
+    for (uint64_t sample = 1; sample <= 238; ++sample) {
+        mockMonoUs = 1000000 + sample * 500000;
+        ctx.frame.freshFrame = true;
+        actualFrameProgress(&ctx, ESP_OK, 1);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    }
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 121);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.guardState == SENSORARRAY_BOOT_GUARD_NORMAL);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartWindowStartUs == 0);
+    assert(sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart(SENSORARRAY_BOOT_RESTART_KIND_AUTO));
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 1);
+
+    /* A valid frame after a 120-second complete stall cannot reset budget. */
+    setExhaustedBudget();
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 1000);
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 1120);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.guardState == SENSORARRAY_BOOT_GUARD_RECOVERY_SAFE);
+
+    /* Sparse observations restart qualification; neither 128 samples nor a
+     * long wall time can conceal >30-second gaps. This is not a scan fault. */
+    setExhaustedBudget();
+    for (uint64_t sample = 0; sample < 256; ++sample) {
+        healthyAt(&ctx, 2000 + sample * 31);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    }
+
+    /* Even a continuous 126-second interval has only 127 frames. The next
+     * actual frame is the 128th, and both conditions then permit reset. */
+    setExhaustedBudget();
+    for (uint64_t sample = 0; sample < 127; ++sample) {
+        healthyAt(&ctx, 12000 + sample);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    }
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 12127);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+
+    /* Failed conversion, non-fresh data and no valid data each break the
+     * consecutive health interval through the actual caller expression. */
+    for (unsigned failure = 0; failure < 3; ++failure) {
+        setExhaustedBudget();
+        uint64_t start = 15000 + failure * 1000;
+        for (uint64_t sample = 0; sample < 120; ++sample) {
+            healthyAt(&ctx, start + sample);
+        }
+        mockMonoUs = (start + 120) * 1000000;
+        ctx.frame.freshFrame = failure != 1;
+        actualFrameProgress(&ctx, failure == 0 ? ESP_FAIL : ESP_OK,
+                            failure == 2 ? 0 : 1);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+        for (uint64_t sample = 0; sample < 127; ++sample) {
+            healthyAt(&ctx, start + 121 + sample);
+            assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+        }
+        healthyAt(&ctx, start + 248);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+    }
+
+    /* READY breaks the current healthy interval and preserves budget. */
+    setExhaustedBudget();
+    for (uint64_t sample = 0; sample < 120; ++sample) {
+        healthyAt(&ctx, 20000 + sample);
+    }
+    actualReadyBlock(&ctx, ESP_OK, ESP_OK);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    for (uint64_t sample = 0; sample < 127; ++sample) {
+        healthyAt(&ctx, 20120 + sample);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    }
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 20247);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+
+    /* A new monotonic epoch cannot make subtraction pass immediately. */
+    setExhaustedBudget();
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 25000);
+    for (uint64_t sample = 1; sample <= 127; ++sample) {
+        healthyAt(&ctx, sample);
+        assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 3);
+    }
+    healthyAt(&ctx, 128);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+
+    setExhaustedBudget();
+    mockResetReason = ESP_RST_POWERON;
+    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbStart();
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.autoRestartCount == 0);
+    assert(s_bootBreadcrumb.guardState == SENSORARRAY_BOOT_GUARD_NORMAL);
+    testRecoveryIdentity();
+    puts("RESTART_BUDGET_PASS,productionFunctions=7,readyBlock=actual,frameCaller=actual,maxAuto=3,slowBootWindowReset=0,stableHealthyUs=120000000,minHealthyFrames=128,maxGapUs=30000000,stallAndSparseDoNotClear=1,failBreaksStreak=1,clockRollback=1,powerOnReset=1");
+    return 0;
+}

+static int testRowsControlAckAndTerminal(void)
+{
+    char response[256];
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigHandleCommand("ROWS=3",6,response,sizeof(response)) == ESP_OK);
+    unsigned long requestId=0;
+    CHECK(sscanf(response,"RCMD,id=%lu",&requestId)==1);
+    sensorarrayCommand_t command;
+    CHECK(!sensorarrayCommandMailboxTryReceive(&command));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxCommitConfigAck((uint32_t)requestId));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxTryReceive(&command));
+    CHECK(command.type==SENSORARRAY_COMMAND_ROWS && command.value==3u);
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigStageAcceptedRows(3u,command.requestId)==ESP_OK);
+    sensorarrayScanConfigApplyResult_t applied;
+    sensorarrayScanConfigApplyPendingAtFrameBoundary(&applied);
+    CHECK(applied.applied && applied.requestId==command.requestId);
+    sensorarrayFrameConfigSnapshot_t snapshot=sensorarrayScanConfigGetFrameSnapshot();
+    CHECK(snapshot.rows==3u && snapshot.lastTerminal && snapshot.lastApplied);
+    CHECK(snapshot.requestId==snapshot.lastRequestId);
+    const char terminal[]="RAPP,id=1,status=applied\n";
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxEmitConfigTerminal(command.requestId,terminal,strlen(terminal))==ESP_OK);
+    char drained[SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_GUARANTEED_TEXT_MAX];size_t length;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayTransportGuaranteedTextTakeDrain(drained,sizeof(drained),&length));
+    return 0;
+}

+static int testModeAndRowsShareArbiter(void)
+{
+    uint32_t id = 0u, other = 0u;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxPostMeasurementMode(SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE, &id) == ESP_OK);
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxPostRowModes("VVVVVVVV", &other) != ESP_OK);
+    sensorarrayScanConfigRequestResult_t rows;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigRequestRows(1u, &rows) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    sensorarrayCommand_t command;
+    CHECK(!sensorarrayCommandMailboxTryReceive(&command));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxCommitConfigAck(id));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxTryReceive(&command));
+    CHECK(command.type == SENSORARRAY_COMMAND_MEASUREMENT_MODE && command.requestId == id);
+    const char terminal[] = "MAPP,id=1,state=applied\n";
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxEmitConfigTerminal(id, terminal, strlen(terminal)) == ESP_OK);
+    char drained[SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_GUARANTEED_TEXT_MAX]; size_t length;
+    CHECK(sensorarrayTransportGuaranteedTextTakeDrain(drained, sizeof(drained), &length));
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigRequestRows(1u, &rows) == ESP_OK);
+    CHECK(rows.requestId != id);
+    CHECK(sensorarrayCommandMailboxPostMeasurementMode(SENSORARRAY_MEASUREMENT_MODE_CAPACITANCE, &other) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigRequestRows(8u, NULL) == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE);
+    sensorarrayScanConfigApplyResult_t applied;
+    sensorarrayScanConfigApplyPendingAtFrameBoundary(&applied);
+    CHECK(applied.applied && applied.requestId == rows.requestId);
+    CHECK(sensorarrayScanConfigGetFrameSnapshot().requestId == rows.requestId);

+#include <assert.h>
+#include <setjmp.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
+#include "common.h"
+static int64_t nowUs;
+static unsigned allocationCalls, allocationsToFail;
+static bool psramUnavailable;
+static int installCount, clearCount, writeBudget = -1, scenario, readCount;
+static bool installed;
+static void (*txTask)(void *);
+static bool taskEntered;
+static jmp_buf taskExit;
+static char output[20000];
+static size_t outputLength;
+static esp_vfs_t registeredVfs;
+
+void *heap_caps_calloc(size_t count, size_t size, uint32_t caps)
+{
+    allocationCalls++;
+    if (allocationsToFail) { allocationsToFail--; return NULL; }
+    if (psramUnavailable && (caps & MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM)) { return NULL; }
+    assert(caps == (MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT) ||
+           caps == (MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT));
+    return calloc(count, size);
+}
+
+QueueHandle_t xQueueCreateStatic(unsigned count, unsigned size, uint8_t *bytes, StaticQueue_t *queue)
+{
+    assert(size == 1); *queue = (StaticQueue_t){.length=count, .data=bytes}; return queue;
+}
+int xQueueSend(QueueHandle_t q, const void *item, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    assert(ticks == 0); if (q->count == q->length) { return 0; }
+    q->data[q->write++ % q->length] = *(const uint8_t *)item; q->count++; return 1;
+}
+int xQueueReceive(QueueHandle_t q, void *item, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    assert(ticks == 0); if (!q->count) { return 0; }
+    *(uint8_t *)item = q->data[q->read++ % q->length]; q->count--; return 1;
+}
+int xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(void (*fn)(void *), const char *name, unsigned stack, void *arg, unsigned prio, TaskHandle_t *task, int core)
+{
+    txTask = fn; *task = (void *)1; return pdPASS;
+}
+int xTaskNotifyGive(TaskHandle_t task) { return 1; }
+uint32_t ulTaskNotifyTake(int clear, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    if (taskEntered) { longjmp(taskExit, 1); }
+    taskEntered = true; return 1;
+}
+void vTaskDelay(TickType_t ticks) { nowUs += ticks * 1000; }
+unsigned uxTaskGetStackHighWaterMark(TaskHandle_t task) { return 2048; }
+int64_t esp_timer_get_time(void) { return nowUs; }
+esp_err_t usb_serial_jtag_driver_install(usb_serial_jtag_driver_config_t *config)
+{
+    installed = true; installCount++; return ESP_OK;
+}
+bool usb_serial_jtag_is_driver_installed(void) { return installed; }
+bool usb_serial_jtag_is_connected(void) { return scenario != 1; }
+int usb_serial_jtag_write_bytes(const void *data, size_t length, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    assert(ticks == 0 && length <= 64);
+    if (scenario == 2 && nowUs >= 20000) { writeBudget = -1; }
+    if (writeBudget == 0) { return 0; }
+    if (writeBudget > 0 && length > (size_t)writeBudget) { length = (size_t)writeBudget; }
+    memcpy(output + outputLength, data, length); outputLength += length;
+    if (writeBudget > 0) { writeBudget -= (int)length; }
+    return (int)length;
+}
+int usb_serial_jtag_read_bytes(void *data, uint32_t length, TickType_t ticks)
+{
+    assert(ticks > 0); memcpy(data, "MODE?\n", 6); readCount++; return 6;
+}
+esp_err_t usb_serial_jtag_wait_tx_done(TickType_t ticks) { assert(ticks == 0); return ESP_OK; }
+esp_err_t esp_vfs_register(const char *path, const esp_vfs_t *vfs, void *context) { registeredVfs = *vfs; return ESP_OK; }
+FILE *test_freopen(const char *path, const char *mode, FILE *stream) { return stream; }
+int test_setvbuf(FILE *stream, char *buffer, int mode, size_t size) { return 0; }
+void test_clearerr(FILE *stream) { clearCount++; }
+
+#include "../../core/transport/sensorarraySerialIo.c"
+
+static void drain(void)
+{
+    taskEntered = false;
+    if (setjmp(taskExit) == 0) { txTask(NULL); }
+}
+int main(int argc, char **argv)
+{
+    const char *allocation = argc > 1 ? argv[1] : "psram";
+    psramUnavailable = strcmp(allocation, "internal") == 0;
+    if (strcmp(allocation, "failure_retry") == 0) {
+        allocationsToFail = 2;
+        assert(sensorarraySerialIoInit() == ESP_ERR_NO_MEM);
+        assert(installCount == 0 && txTask == NULL && s_dataBytes == NULL);
+    }
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoInit() == ESP_OK);
+    unsigned allocationsAfterInit = allocationCalls;
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoInit() == ESP_OK && installCount == 1);
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("DATA\n", 5, false) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("MAPP\n", 5, true) == ESP_OK);
+    drain();
+    assert(outputLength == 10 && memcmp(output, "MAPP\nDATA\n", 10) == 0);
+    /* DATA floods cannot use reserved priority slots. */
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) { assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("D\n", 2, false) == ESP_OK); }
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("DROP\n", 5, false) == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT);
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("CTRL\n", 5, true) == ESP_OK);
+    drain();
+    assert(clearCount >= 4);
+    /* No host: bounded TX + a still functional independent command RX. */
+    scenario = 1; writeBudget = 0; nowUs = 0;
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("DATA\n", 5, false) == ESP_OK);
+    drain(); assert(nowUs == 20000);
+    char rx[64]; assert(sensorarraySerialIoRead(rx, sizeof(rx)) == 6 && readCount == 1);
+    sensorarraySerialIoStats_t stats;
+    sensorarraySerialIoGetStats(&stats);
+    assert(stats.normalBufferBytes == 5u * SENSORARRAY_TRANSPORT_TEXT_MAX);
+    assert(stats.normalBufferMemory == (psramUnavailable ? SENSORARRAY_SERIAL_MEMORY_INTERNAL : SENSORARRAY_SERIAL_MEMORY_PSRAM));
+    assert(stats.timeout == 1 && stats.rxBytes == 6 && !stats.sofPresent);
+    /* Real partial driver return: retain prefix count, discard remainder,
+     * delimit next packet, then resume once host reads again. */
+    scenario = 2; writeBudget = 80; nowUs = 0; outputLength = 0;
+    char packet[160]; memset(packet, 'A', sizeof(packet));
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue(packet, sizeof(packet), false) == ESP_OK);
+    assert(sensorarraySerialIoQueue("AFTER\n", 6, false) == ESP_OK);
+    drain();
+    assert(outputLength == 87 && memcmp(output + 80, "\nAFTER\n", 7) == 0);
+    sensorarraySerialIoGetStats(&stats);
+    assert(stats.partialWrite == 1 && stats.timeout == 2 && stats.driverAcceptedBytes > 80);
+    /* The actual registered VFS reports full queue rejection, never fake success. */
+    scenario = 0; writeBudget = -1;
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) { assert(registeredVfs.write(0, "LOG\n", 4) == 4); }
+    assert(registeredVfs.write(0, "LOG\n", 4) == -1 && errno == EAGAIN);
+    drain(); assert(registeredVfs.write(0, "RECOVERED\n", 10) == 10); drain();
+    assert(allocationCalls == allocationsAfterInit); /* no hot path allocations */
+    printf("SERIAL_IO_PASS,allocation=%s,initOnce=1,noHotAlloc=1,priority=1,noHostBoundUs=20000,rxIndependent=1,partialRecovery=1,vfsRejectAndResume=1\n", allocation);
+    return 0;
+}

+#include <assert.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "sensorarrayTransportFrameMeta.h"
+
+static void parse(sensorarrayTransportPayloadSlot_t *slot, const char *data)
+{
+    strcpy(slot->data, data);
+    slot->length = (uint16_t)strlen(data);
+    sensorarrayTransportParseFrameMeta(slot);
+}
+int main(void)
+{
+    sensorarrayTransportPayloadSlot_t slot = {0};
+    parse(&slot, "C,seq=11,rows=1,cells=8,mgen=4,mrid=3,gen=2,rid=1,session=0123456789ABCDEF,bootId=99,rev=6,rgen=2,rrid=1,pgen=5,prid=4\nK,gen=999\n");
+    assert(slot.hasFrameMeta && slot.generation == 2 && slot.requestId == 1);
+    assert(slot.modeGeneration == 4 && slot.modeRequestId == 3);
+    assert(slot.rowsGeneration == 2 && slot.rowsRequestId == 1);
+    assert(slot.profileGeneration == 5 && slot.profileRequestId == 4);
+    assert(slot.sessionId == UINT64_C(0x0123456789abcdef) && slot.bootId == 99 && slot.configRevision == 6);
+    /* Metadata stays attached to queued payload; no global-state restamping. */
+    sensorarrayTransportPayloadSlot_t queued = slot;
+    parse(&slot, "V,seq=12,rows=1,cells=8,gen=7,rid=6,mgen=7,mrid=6,rgen=2,rrid=1\n");
+    assert(queued.modeGeneration == 4 && slot.modeGeneration == 7);
+    parse(&slot, "M,seq=13,rows=1,cells=8,pgen=8,prid=7,rgen=2,rrid=1,mgen=9,mrid=8\n");
+    assert(slot.generation == 2 && slot.requestId == 1 && slot.profileGeneration == 8);
+    parse(&slot, "C,seq=14,rows=1,cells=8,mgen=77,mrid=66\nK,gen=22,rid=11\n");
+    assert(slot.generation == 0 && slot.requestId == 0 && slot.modeGeneration == 77);
+    char preview[384];
+    parse(&slot, "C,seq=15,rows=1,cells=8,gen=2,rid=1,mgen=77,mrid=66,rgen=2,rrid=1,pgen=3,prid=4,session=0123456789abcdef,bootId=99,rev=5\nD,42,43\n");
+    assert(sensorarrayTransportBuildPreview(&slot, preview, sizeof(preview)));
+    assert(strstr(preview, "previewOnly=1,gen=2,rid=1,session=0123456789ABCDEF,bootId=99,rev=5,mgen=77,mrid=66,rgen=2,rrid=1,pgen=3,prid=4"));
+    assert(strstr(preview, "d0=42,dl=43"));
+    parse(&slot, "C,seq=16,rows=1,cells=8,mgen=1\nD,X01,X01\n");
+    assert(sensorarrayTransportBuildPreview(&slot, preview, sizeof(preview)));
+    assert(strstr(preview, "d0=na,dl=na"));
+    parse(&slot, "C,seq=4294967296,rows=1,cells=8,gen=1,rid=1\n");
+    assert(!slot.hasFrameMeta);
+    puts("TRANSPORT_FRAME_METADATA_PASS,domains=rows;mode;profile;session,exact_fields=1,immutable=1");
+}

+def source_code(path: Path) -> str:
+    text = load_text(path)
+    if path.suffix in C_SOURCE_EXTS:
+        return C_NON_CODE_RE.sub(
+            lambda match: "".join("\n" if char == "\n" else " " for char in match.group()),
+            text,
+        )
+    return text

+def find_sdk_root() -> Path | None:
+    candidates: list[Path] = []
+    if os.environ.get("IDF_PATH"):
+        candidates.append(Path(os.environ["IDF_PATH"]))
+    try:
+        description = json.loads(load_text(ROOT / "build" / "project_description.json"))
+        if isinstance(description, dict) and description.get("idf_path"):
+            candidates.append(Path(description["idf_path"]))
+    except (ValueError, TypeError):
+        pass
+    for candidate in candidates:
+        if (candidate / "components").is_dir() and (candidate / "Kconfig").is_file():
+            return candidate
+    return None
+
+
+def collect_sdk_defined(sdk_root: Path | None) -> set[str]:
+    """Recognise declared SDK symbols; enabled values are not declarations."""
+    defined: set[str] = set()
+    if sdk_root is not None:
+        paths = [sdk_root / "Kconfig"]
+        paths.extend((sdk_root / "components").rglob("Kconfig*"))
+        for path in paths:
+            if not path.is_file():
+                continue
+            for line in load_text(path).splitlines():
+                match = KCONFIG_DEF_RE.match(line)
+                if match:
+                    defined.add(match.group(1))
+    return defined
+

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Run synthetic time/SPI/DRDY failures against the real ADS driver source."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import os
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+
+
+def main():
+    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+    compiler = os.environ.get("CC") or shutil.which("gcc") or shutil.which("clang")
+    if not compiler:
+        raise RuntimeError("host C compiler required")
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-ads-driver-") as temp:
+        binary = Path(temp) / "test_ads_driver"
+        subprocess.run([
+            compiler, "-std=c11", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror",
+            "-ffunction-sections", "-fdata-sections", "-Wl,--gc-sections",
+            "-I", str(root / "tests/host/ads_stubs"),
+            "-I", str(root / "tests/host/include"),
+            "-I", str(root / "components/ads126xAdc/include"),
+            str(root / "tests/host/test_ads_driver_reliability.c"),
+            "-o", str(binary),
+        ], check=True)
+        subprocess.run([str(binary)], check=True)
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Generate/verify synthetic fault replays through the production C formatter."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import argparse
+import os
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument("--update", action="store_true", help="regenerate tracked synthetic fixtures")
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+    compiler = os.environ.get("CC") or shutil.which("gcc") or shutil.which("clang")
+    if not compiler:
+        raise RuntimeError("host C compiler required")
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-ads-fixtures-") as tmp:
+        binary = Path(tmp) / "emit_ads_fault_fixture"
+        subprocess.run([
+            compiler, "-std=c11", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-pedantic",
+            "-I", str(root / "tests/host/include"),
+            "-I", str(root / "core/measure/ads"),
+            str(root / "tests/host/emit_ads_fault_fixture.c"),
+            str(root / "core/measure/ads/sensorarrayAdsFault.c"),
+            "-o", str(binary),
+        ], check=True)
+        output = subprocess.check_output([str(binary)], text=True)
+    count = 0
+    for body in output.split("# fixture=")[1:]:
+        name = body.split(";", 1)[0]
+        text = "# fixture=" + body
+        path = root / "tests/fixtures/ads_fault" / (name.lower() + ".synthetic.log")
+        records = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
+        assert len(records) == 8, (name, len(records))
+        assert all(len(line.encode("ascii")) < 384 for line in records[1:])
+        fields = dict(part.split("=", 1) for part in records[1].strip().split(",")[1:])
+        assert fields["stage"] == name and fields["mode"] == "VOLT"
+        assert fields["route"] == "VOLT" and fields["owner"] == "MATRIX"
+        assert fields["err"] == ("0x107" if name == "REGISTER_HEALTH_READ" else "0x108")
+        assert "attempted=0,acquired=0" in text
+        assert "railSpanUv=5200000,avddUv=3400000,avssUv=-1800000" in text
+        if name in ("POWER_COMPARE", "RESTORE_VERIFY"):
+            assert "originalError=0x108,cleanupError=0x103" in text
+        if args.update:
+            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+            path.write_text(text)
+        else:
+            assert path.read_text() == text, f"fixture drift: {path}"
+        count += 1
+    assert count == 10
+    print(f"ADS_FAULT_FIXTURES,passed=1,count={count},fixture=synthetic,producer=actual_C_formatter")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Regression tests for false config identifiers and real undefined symbols."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import contextlib
+import io
+import json
+import os
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import audit_config_surface as audit
+
+
+class ConfigSurfaceTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self) -> None:
+        self.temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-config-surface-")
+        self.addCleanup(self.temporary.cleanup)
+        base = Path(self.temporary.name)
+        self.root = base / "project"
+        self.sdk = base / "sdk"
+        (self.root / "main").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (self.root / "docs/audit").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (self.root / "build").mkdir()
+        (self.sdk / "components/esp_psram/esp32s3").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (self.sdk / "Kconfig").write_text("menuconfig SDK_MENU\n    bool\n")
+        (self.sdk / "components/esp_psram/esp32s3/Kconfig.spiram").write_text(
+            "config SPIRAM\n    bool\n"
+        )
+        self.kconfig = self.root / "main/Kconfig.projbuild"
+        self.kconfig.write_text("config SENSORARRAY_KNOWN\n    bool\n")
+        (self.root / "CMakeLists.txt").write_text("if(CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_KNOWN)\nendif()\n")
+        (self.root / "build/project_description.json").write_text(
+            json.dumps({"idf_path": str(self.sdk)})
+        )
+        (self.root / "docs/audit/run.txt").write_text(
+            "CONFIG_IDENTITY_TESTS,passed=1\nCONFIG_SENSORARRAY_DOC_LOG\n"
+        )
+        self.source = self.root / "main/main.c"
+        self.source.write_text(
+            r'''// CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_COMMENT
+/* CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_BLOCK
+ * #ifndef CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_FAKE_FALLBACK
+ */
+const char *message = "CONFIG_TERM_DROP,escaped=\"CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_QUOTED\"";
+const char *multiline = "CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_LITERAL\
+ CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_CONTINUED_LITERAL";
+int character = 'CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_CHARACTER';
+// Continued comment \
+CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_CONTINUED_COMMENT
+#if CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_KNOWN && CONFIG_SPIRAM && CONFIG_SDK_MENU
+#endif
+'''
+        )
+        self.addCleanup(patch.stopall)
+        patch.object(audit, "ROOT", self.root).start()
+        patch.object(audit, "SCAN_FILES", [self.kconfig]).start()
+        patch.dict(os.environ, {"IDF_PATH": str(base / "missing-sdk")}).start()
+
+    def run_audit(self) -> tuple[int, str]:
+        output = io.StringIO()
+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output):
+            code = audit.main()
+        return code, output.getvalue()
+
+    def test_literals_comments_and_document_logs_are_not_macros(self) -> None:
+        self.assertEqual(set(audit.collect_used()), {"SENSORARRAY_KNOWN", "SPIRAM", "SDK_MENU"})
+        self.assertEqual(audit.collect_fallbacks(), {})
+        self.assertNotIn(self.root / "docs/audit/run.txt", audit.iter_source_files())
+        self.assertIn(self.root / "CMakeLists.txt", audit.iter_source_files())
+        self.assertEqual(audit.find_sdk_root(), self.sdk)
+        code, output = self.run_audit()
+        self.assertEqual(code, 0, output)
+        self.assertIn("usedButNotDefined: 0", output)
+
+    def test_real_undefined_project_macro_still_fails_with_correct_line(self) -> None:
+        text = self.source.read_text()
+        line = len(text.splitlines()) + 1
+        self.source.write_text(text + "#if CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_REAL_MISSING\n#endif\n")
+        self.assertEqual(audit.collect_used()["SENSORARRAY_REAL_MISSING"], [f"main/main.c:{line}"])
+        code, output = self.run_audit()
+        self.assertEqual(code, 1, output)
+        self.assertIn("usedButNotDefined: 1\n  SENSORARRAY_REAL_MISSING", output)
+
+    def test_sdk_definitions_do_not_exempt_undefined_spiram_names(self) -> None:
+        self.source.write_text("#if CONFIG_SPIRAM_UNDECLARED\n#endif\n")
+        code, output = self.run_audit()
+        self.assertEqual(code, 1, output)
+        self.assertIn("SPIRAM_UNDECLARED", output)
+
+    def test_no_sdk_uses_only_exact_spiram_compatibility_name(self) -> None:
+        (self.root / "build/project_description.json").unlink()
+        self.source.write_text("#if CONFIG_SPIRAM\n#endif\n")
+        self.assertIsNone(audit.find_sdk_root())
+        code, output = self.run_audit()
+        self.assertEqual(code, 0, output)
+        self.source.write_text("#if CONFIG_SPIRAM_UNDECLARED || CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_REAL_MISSING\n#endif\n")
+        code, output = self.run_audit()
+        self.assertEqual(code, 1, output)
+        self.assertIn("usedButNotDefined: 2", output)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Execute firmware identity/encoder regressions; generated fixtures are synthetic."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import os
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+import zlib
+from test_rowmodes_ack_guard import findCompiler, writeFakeHeaders
+
+
+def main():
+    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+    compiler, native = findCompiler()
+    if not native:
+        raise RuntimeError("identity regression requires a host compiler; compile-only is not PASS")
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-identity-") as temporary:
+        temp = Path(temporary)
+        fake = temp / "fake"
+        fake.mkdir()
+        writeFakeHeaders(fake)
+        (fake / "sdkconfig.h").write_text("#pragma once\n")
+        includes = [fake, root / "tests/host/frame_stubs", root / "tests/host/include",
+                    root / "main", root / "main/output", root / "core/measure/include",
+                    root / "core/config", root / "core/boardSupport/include",
+                    root / "components/ads126xAdc/include", root / "components/fdc2214Cap/include",
+                    root / "components/tmuxSwitch/include"]
+        sources = [root / p for p in ["tests/host/test_config_identity.c",
+                   "core/measure/sensorarrayConfigIdentity.c", "core/measure/sensorarrayMeasurementMode.c",
+                   "core/measure/sensorarrayRowModeProfile.c", "core/measure/sensorarrayFrameBuilder.c",
+                   "main/output/sensorarrayTextProtocol.c"]]
+        executable = temp / "test_identity"
+        command = [compiler, "-std=c11", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror"]
+        for include in includes:
+            command.extend(["-I", str(include)])
+        command += [str(p) for p in sources] + ["-lm", "-o", str(executable)]
+        subprocess.run(command, check=True)
+        fixture_dir = root / "tests/fixtures/config_identity"
+        fixture_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        result = subprocess.run([str(executable), str(fixture_dir)], check=False, text=True, capture_output=True)
+        print(result.stdout, end="")
+        print(result.stderr, end="")
+        result.check_returncode()
+        count = 0
+        for path in sorted(fixture_dir.glob("*.frame")):
+            data = path.read_bytes()
+            body, trailer = data.rsplit(b"K,", 1)
+            fields = dict(field.split(b"=", 1) for field in trailer.strip().split(b",") if b"=" in field)
+            assert int(fields[b"crc"], 16) == zlib.crc32(body), path
+            header = dict(field.split(b"=", 1) for field in body.splitlines()[0].split(b",") if b"=" in field)
+            for key in (b"session", b"bootId", b"rev"):
+                assert header[key] == fields[key], (path, key)
+            count += 1
+        print(f"ENCODER_GOLDEN_CRC,passed={count},provenance=synthetic_real_encoder")
+        def fields(path):
+            first = path.read_text().splitlines()[0]
+            return dict(token.split("=", 1) for token in first.split(",")[1:] if "=" in token)
+        def identity_matches(frame, authority):
+            return all(frame[key] == authority[key] for key in
+                       ("session", "bootId", "rev", "mgen", "mrid", "rgen", "rrid", "pgen", "prid"))
+        before = fields(fixture_dir / "cap_before.frame")
+        volt = fields(fixture_dir / "volt_transition.frame")
+        after = fields(fixture_dir / "cap_after.frame")
+        reapplied = fields(fixture_dir / "cap_reapplied.frame")
+        state_before = fields(fixture_dir / "cap_before.config")
+        state_after = fields(fixture_dir / "cap_after.config")
+        state_reapplied = fields(fixture_dir / "cap_reapplied.config")
+        # Reproduce E5: C.gen=2 is ROWS, V.gen=3 is MODE; C.mgen=4.
+        assert after["gen"] == after["rgen"] == "2"
+        assert volt["gen"] == volt["mgen"] == "3"
+        assert after["mgen"] == "4"
+        # Missing terminal / frame before terminal: old authority cannot stamp
+        # the frame. A side-effect-free query resolves the actual tuple.
+        assert not identity_matches(after, state_before)
+        assert identity_matches(after, state_after)
+        # Late old terminal/frame and the same CAP reapplied cannot roll back.
+        assert not identity_matches(before, state_reapplied)
+        assert not identity_matches(after, state_reapplied)
+        assert identity_matches(reapplied, state_reapplied)
+        assert state_after["mtermId"] == after["mrid"] and state_after["mterm"] == "applied"
+        print("CONFIG_RECOVERY_REPLAYS,passed=5,provenance=synthetic")
+    return 0
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Run the actual worker/coordinator functions in a pthread FreeRTOS shim.
+
+Hardware calls are synthetic, explicitly injected delays/failures. Production
+worker/coordinator code is extracted verbatim, not reimplemented in this test.
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+source = (root / "core/measure/fdc/sensorarrayFdcRowEpoch.c").read_text()
+worker = source[source.index("bool sensorarrayMeasureFdcWorkersQuiescent"):source.index("esp_err_t sensorarrayMeasureEnsureFdcWorkers")]
+start = source.index("esp_err_t sensorarrayMeasureReadFdcMatrixRowParallelEpoch(")
+coordinator = source[start:source.index("esp_err_t __attribute__((unused)) sensorarrayMeasureReadFdcRuntimeChannelConfigs", start)]
+header = (root / "core/measure/fdc/sensorarrayFdcInternal.h").read_text()
+def struct(name):
+    end = header.index("} " + name + ";") + len("} " + name + ";")
+    begin = header.rfind("typedef struct {", 0, end)
+    return header[begin:end]
+row_fields = sorted(set(re.findall(r"rowTiming->(\w+)", coordinator)))
+device_fields = sorted(set(re.findall(r"(?:primaryTiming|secondaryTiming)->(\w+)", coordinator)) | {"row", "deviceId"})
+prelude = (root / "tests/host/fdc_worker_runtime_prelude.h").read_text()
+prelude = prelude.replace("/* ROW_FIELDS */", "\n".join("uint64_t " + k + ";" for k in row_fields))
+prelude = prelude.replace("/* DEVICE_FIELDS */", "\n".join("uint64_t " + k + ";" for k in device_fields))
+prelude = prelude.replace("/* FIRMWARE_TYPES */", "\n".join(struct(n) for n in ["sensorarrayFdcWorkerResult_t", "sensorarrayFdcWorkerTrace_t", "sensorarrayFdcFrameReadTracker_t", "sensorarrayFdcWorkerJob_t"]))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-fdc-runtime-") as temp:
+    temp = Path(temp)
+    unit = temp / "worker_runtime.c"
+    unit.write_text(prelude + "\n" + worker + "\n" + coordinator + "\n" + (root / "tests/host/fdc_worker_runtime_main.c").read_text())
+    exe = temp / "test"
+    subprocess.run([os.environ.get("CC", "cc"), "-std=c11", "-O1", "-g", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-Wno-unused-parameter", "-pthread", "-fsanitize=address,undefined", "-fno-omit-frame-pointer", "-I", str(root / "core/measure/fdc"), str(unit), "-o", str(exe)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([str(exe)], check=True, timeout=20, env={**os.environ, "ASAN_OPTIONS": "detect_leaks=0:detect_stack_use_after_return=1"})

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Compile actual main.c commit/terminal code with injected hardware boundaries."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from test_rowmodes_ack_guard import findCompiler, writeFakeHeaders
+
+
+def function(source, name):
+    start = source.index(name)
+    opening = source.index("{", start)
+    depth = 1
+    end = opening + 1
+    while depth:
+        depth += (source[end] == "{") - (source[end] == "}")
+        end += 1
+    return source[start:end]
+
+
+def main():
+    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+    source = (root / "main/main.c").read_text()
+    # Exact production branches: no hand-maintained mirror of decisions.
+    start = source.index("        case SENSORARRAY_COMMAND_MEASUREMENT_MODE:\n")
+    end = source.index("        case SENSORARRAY_COMMAND_ROW_MODES:", start)
+    mode_case = source[start:end]
+    publish_start = source.index("/* 本轮新增: only the acquisition owner publishes")
+    publish = function(source[publish_start:], "static sensorarrayConfigIdentity_t sensorarrayPublishConfigAuthority(")
+    terminal_start = source.index("static void sensorarrayEmitConfigTerminal(", source.index("static esp_err_t sensorarrayApplyMeasurementMode("))
+    terminal = function(source[terminal_start:], "static void sensorarrayEmitConfigTerminal(")
+    mixed = function(source, "else if (mixedRequestFailed)")
+    mixed_body = mixed[mixed.index("{"):]
+    harness = (root / "tests/host/test_main_config_commit.c").read_text()
+    harness = harness.replace("/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_PUBLISH */", publish)
+    harness = harness.replace("/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_TERMINAL */", terminal)
+    harness = harness.replace("/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_MODE_CASE */", mode_case)
+    harness = harness.replace("/* INSERT_PRODUCTION_MIXED_FAILURE */", mixed_body)
+    compiler, native = findCompiler()
+    if not native:
+        raise RuntimeError("a native host compiler is required")
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-main-config-") as temporary:
+        temp = Path(temporary)
+        fake = temp / "fake"
+        fake.mkdir()
+        writeFakeHeaders(fake)
+        generated = temp / "test.c"
+        generated.write_text(harness)
+        executable = temp / "test"
+        command = [compiler, "-std=c11", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror"]
+        for include in (fake, root / "tests/host/include", root / "core/measure/include", root / "core/config"):
+            command += ["-I", str(include)]
+        command += [str(generated), str(root / "core/measure/sensorarrayMeasurementMode.c"),
+                    str(root / "core/measure/sensorarrayRowModeProfile.c"),
+                    str(root / "core/measure/sensorarrayConfigIdentity.c"),
+                    str(root / "core/config/sensorarrayScanConfig.c"), "-o", str(executable)]
+        subprocess.run(command, check=True)
+        subprocess.run([str(executable)], check=True)
+    return 0
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Compile actual RTC restart-budget functions and READY/frame call sites.
+
+Clock, RTC storage, heap and event sinks are host shims. No device reset occurs.
+The current production name is sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart (not the audit
+prompt's sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbPrepareRestart).
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+
+root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+source = (root / "main/main.c").read_text()
+
+def function(name: str) -> str:
+    match = re.search(r"^static\s+[^\n]+\b" + re.escape(name) + r"\(", source, re.M)
+    if not match:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"production function missing: {name}")
+    opening = source.index("{", match.start())
+    # These functions contain no literal brace characters in strings. Refuse to
+    # silently synthesize a replacement if production source changes shape.
+    depth = 1
+    end = opening + 1
+    while depth:
+        depth += (source[end] == "{") - (source[end] == "}")
+        end += 1
+    return source[match.start():end]
+
+begin = source.index("#define SENSORARRAY_BOOT_BREADCRUMB_MAGIC")
+end = source.index("#define SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_LEVEL_NONE", begin)
+rtc_types = source[begin:end]
+ready_begin = source.index("    ctx->systemReady = initErr == ESP_OK && bootErr == ESP_OK;")
+ready_end = source.index("    sensorarrayFdcMapVerifyDebug();", ready_begin)
+ready = source[ready_begin:ready_end]
+frame_match = re.search(r"    sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbMarkStableProgress\(\s*err == ESP_OK[^;]+;", source)
+if not frame_match:
+    raise RuntimeError("actual healthy frame caller missing")
+
+prelude = r'''
+#include <assert.h>
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <stdarg.h>
+#include <stdint.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+typedef int esp_err_t;
+#define ESP_OK 0
+#define ESP_FAIL -1
+#define RTC_NOINIT_ATTR
+#define portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED 0
+typedef int portMUX_TYPE;
+static unsigned lockDepth;
+#define portENTER_CRITICAL(p) do { (void)(p); assert(lockDepth == 0); lockDepth++; } while (0)
+#define portEXIT_CRITICAL(p) do { (void)(p); assert(lockDepth == 1); lockDepth--; } while (0)
+typedef enum {ESP_RST_POWERON, ESP_RST_SW} esp_reset_reason_t;
+typedef struct {
+    struct { uint32_t sequence; bool freshFrame; } frame;
+    struct { unsigned quality; } fdcBootSummary;
+    bool systemReady;
+} sensorarrayAppContext_t;
+static uint64_t mockMonoUs, mockRtcUs;
+static esp_reset_reason_t mockResetReason;
+static unsigned readyEvents;
+static int64_t esp_timer_get_time(void) { return (int64_t)mockMonoUs; }
+static uint64_t esp_rtc_get_time_us(void) { return mockRtcUs; }
+static esp_reset_reason_t esp_reset_reason(void) { return mockResetReason; }
+static uint32_t esp_get_free_heap_size(void) { return 123456; }
+static const char *sensorarrayResetReasonName(esp_reset_reason_t r) { return r == ESP_RST_POWERON ? "synthetic_poweron" : "synthetic_software"; }
+static const char *sensorarrayAppFdcBootQualityName(unsigned q) { return "synthetic_ok"; }
+static esp_err_t sensorarrayAsyncLogPublishProtocolEvent(const char *text, size_t length)
+{
+    assert(length > 6 && strncmp(text, "READY,", 6) == 0); readyEvents++; return ESP_OK;
+}
+'''
+functions = "\n\n".join(function(name) for name in [
+    "sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbSetStage", "sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbStart",
+    "sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbCopy", "sensorarrayBootBreadcrumbMarkStableProgress",
+    "sensorarrayBootPrepareRestart", "sensorarrayRecoveryPost", "sensorarrayRecoveryTake",
+])
+identity_header = (root / "core/measure/include/sensorarrayConfigIdentity.h").read_text()
+identity_types = identity_header[identity_header.index("typedef struct {"):identity_header.index("/* Capture does not claim")]
+identity_types = "typedef unsigned sensorarrayMeasurementState_t, sensorarrayMeasurementMode_t, sensorarrayRowModeProfileState_t, sensorarrayConfigOutcome_t;\n" + identity_types
+recovery_defines = "\n".join(re.findall(r"^#define SENSORARRAY_RECOVERY_[^\n]+", source, re.M))
+recovery_globals = "\n".join(re.findall(r"^static [^\n]+\bs_recoveryRequest\w+[^\n]+", source, re.M))
+identity_shim = r'''
+static sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t mockAuthority;
+static bool mockAuthorityKnown;
+static unsigned staleErrors;
+static char lastLog[512];
+static bool sensorarrayConfigIdentityCopy(sensorarrayConfigAuthority_t *out)
+{
+    assert(lockDepth == 0); /* Never copy the authority inside the request spinlock. */
+    *out = mockAuthority;
+    return mockAuthorityKnown;
+}
+static int testPrintf(const char *format, ...)
+{
+    va_list ap; va_start(ap, format);
+    int length = vsnprintf(lastLog, sizeof(lastLog), format, ap);
+    va_end(ap);
+    if (strstr(lastLog, "reason=stale_config")) { staleErrors++; }
+    return length;
+}
+#define printf testPrintf
+'''
+unit_text = prelude + rtc_types + identity_types + recovery_defines + "\n" + recovery_globals + identity_shim + functions
+unit_text += "\nstatic void actualReadyBlock(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx, esp_err_t initErr, esp_err_t bootErr)\n{\n" + ready + "}\n"
+unit_text += "\nstatic void actualFrameProgress(sensorarrayAppContext_t *ctx, esp_err_t err, uint64_t validMask)\n{\n" + frame_match.group(0) + "\n}\n"
+unit_text += "\n#undef printf\n" + (root / "tests/host/test_restart_budget.c").read_text()
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-restart-budget-") as temp:
+    temp = Path(temp)
+    unit = temp / "restart_budget.c"
+    unit.write_text(unit_text)
+    exe = temp / "test"
+    subprocess.run([os.environ.get("CC", "cc"), "-std=c11", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-Wno-unused-parameter", str(unit), "-o", str(exe)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([str(exe)], check=True)

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Compile the production route controller/board map with fault-injected IO."""
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+
+def main():
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sensorarray-route-') as td:
+        t = Path(td)
+        (t/'driver').mkdir()
+        (t/'esp_timer.h').write_text('#include <stdint.h>\nint64_t esp_timer_get_time(void);\n')
+        (t/'esp_rom_sys.h').write_text('#include <stdint.h>\nvoid esp_rom_delay_us(uint32_t us);\n')
+        (t/'sensorarrayConfig.h').write_text('''#pragma once
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_FDC_SD_GPIO 8
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_ADS_MATRIX_MODE_SETTLE_US 5000
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_ADS_MATRIX_ROW_SETTLE_US 20
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_ADS_DATA_RATE 15
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_ADS_MATRIX_INTERNAL_REF_UV 2500000
+#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_ADS_RES_BBM_ROW_SWITCH_ENABLE 1
+#define CONFIG_TMUX1108_SWITCH_DELAY_US 2

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Execute actual SerialIo queue/TX/VFS code with synthetic driver results."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import os
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="sensorarray-serial-io-") as temp:
+    temp = Path(temp)
+    (temp / "freertos").mkdir()
+    (temp / "driver").mkdir()
+    common = '''#pragma once
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <stddef.h>
+#include <stdint.h>
+#include <sys/types.h>
+#include <sys/stat.h>
+#include "esp_err.h"
+typedef uint32_t TickType_t;
+typedef uint8_t StackType_t;
+typedef int BaseType_t;
+typedef struct { unsigned length, count, read, write; uint8_t *data; } StaticQueue_t;
+typedef StaticQueue_t *QueueHandle_t;
+typedef void *TaskHandle_t;
+typedef int portMUX_TYPE;
+#define portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED 0
+#define portENTER_CRITICAL(p) ((void)(p))
+#define portEXIT_CRITICAL(p) ((void)(p))
+#define pdTRUE 1
+#define pdPASS 1
+#define portMAX_DELAY UINT32_MAX
+#define pdMS_TO_TICKS(n) (n)
+QueueHandle_t xQueueCreateStatic(unsigned count, unsigned size, uint8_t *bytes, StaticQueue_t *queue);
+int xQueueSend(QueueHandle_t queue, const void *item, TickType_t ticks);
+int xQueueReceive(QueueHandle_t queue, void *item, TickType_t ticks);
+int xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(void (*fn)(void *), const char *name, unsigned stack, void *arg, unsigned prio, TaskHandle_t *task, int core);
+int xTaskNotifyGive(TaskHandle_t task);
+uint32_t ulTaskNotifyTake(int clear, TickType_t ticks);
+void vTaskDelay(TickType_t ticks);
+unsigned uxTaskGetStackHighWaterMark(TaskHandle_t task);
+int64_t esp_timer_get_time(void);
+typedef struct { uint32_t tx_buffer_size, rx_buffer_size; } usb_serial_jtag_driver_config_t;
+esp_err_t usb_serial_jtag_driver_install(usb_serial_jtag_driver_config_t *config);
+bool usb_serial_jtag_is_driver_installed(void);
+bool usb_serial_jtag_is_connected(void);
+int usb_serial_jtag_write_bytes(const void *data, size_t length, TickType_t ticks);
+int usb_serial_jtag_read_bytes(void *data, uint32_t length, TickType_t ticks);
+esp_err_t usb_serial_jtag_wait_tx_done(TickType_t ticks);
+#define ESP_VFS_FLAG_DEFAULT 0
+struct esp_vfs {
+    int flags;
+    int (*open)(const char *, int, int);
+    ssize_t (*write)(int, const void *, size_t);
+    int (*close)(int);
+    int (*fstat)(int, struct stat *);
+};
+typedef struct esp_vfs esp_vfs_t;
+esp_err_t esp_vfs_register(const char *path, const esp_vfs_t *vfs, void *context);
+'''
+    (temp / "common.h").write_text(common)
+    for file in ["freertos/FreeRTOS.h", "freertos/task.h", "freertos/queue.h", "driver/usb_serial_jtag.h", "esp_timer.h", "esp_vfs.h"]:
+        (temp / file).write_text('#include "common.h"\n')
+    (temp / "esp_heap_caps.h").write_text("#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#define MALLOC_CAP_8BIT 1u\n#define MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM 2u\n#define MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL 4u\nvoid *heap_caps_calloc(size_t count, size_t size, uint32_t caps);\n")
+    (temp / "sdkconfig.h").write_text("#define CONFIG_SENSORARRAY_OUTPUT_TASK_CORE 0\n")
+    exe = temp / "test"
+    subprocess.run([os.environ.get("CC", "cc"), "-std=c11", "-D_DEFAULT_SOURCE", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-Wno-unused-parameter", "-Dfreopen=test_freopen", "-Dsetvbuf=test_setvbuf", "-Dclearerr=test_clearerr", "-I", str(temp), "-I", str(root / "tests/host/include"), "-I", str(root / "components/sensorarrayWifi/include"), "-I", str(root / "core/transport"), str(root / "tests/host/test_serial_io.c"), "-o", str(exe)], check=True)
+    for allocation in ["psram", "internal", "failure_retry"]:
+        subprocess.run([str(exe), allocation], check=True)
